### [1] [Coordinate and Momentum Distributions of a Small Composite System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27244)
*Dmitry Naplekov,Yury Bezruk,Vladimir Yanovsky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究由可移动外壳和有限数量内部粒子组成的孤立小系统，在总能量和动量守恒条件下，推导出外壳坐标和动量的精确分布，并分析了外壳因内部粒子碰撞而产生的混沌运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解由有限内部粒子驱动的外壳运动规律，与布朗运动对比，探索无需外部介质即可产生的混沌运动及其与系统内部结构的关系。

Method: 在总能量和动量守恒条件下，对可移动外壳与有限内部粒子组成的孤立小系统，推导外壳坐标和动量的精确分布；比较坐标分布与动量分布对粒子和自由度数量的依赖关系，并分析均方根偏差的普适表达式。

Result: 坐标分布由有限个分支组成且可能存在平台区域，依赖于内部粒子数但与其运动维度无关；动量分布依赖于自由度数量。外壳的混沌运动在无外部介质时仍存在，均方根偏差具有依赖于内部粒子数和质量的普适表达式。

Conclusion: 通过对比外壳的坐标分布和动量分布，可以推断系统的内部结构（粒子数和运动维度）；外壳运动的混沌性区别于布朗运动，不需要外部介质即可持续。

Abstract: An isolated small system consisting of a movable shell and a finite number of internal particles is considered. Exact distributions of the shell coordinates and momenta are obtained for various types of internal particle motion under the conditions of conservation of the total energy and momentum of the system. The coordinate distribution consists of a finite number of branches and may contain plateau regions. The momentum distribution corresponds to a non-uniform distribution of energy among the degrees of freedom of the system. It is shown that the coordinate distribution depends on the number of internal particles and remains unchanged when the dimensionality of their motion varies, whereas the momentum distribution depends on the number of degrees of freedom. Consequently, comparison of the coordinate and momentum distributions of the shell makes it possible to draw conclusions about the internal structure of the system. The chaotic motion of the shell caused by impacts from the internal particles is analyzed; unlike Brownian motion, it persists even in the absence of an external medium. A universal expression for the root-mean-square deviation of the shell is obtained explicitly, depending on the number of internal particles and their masses.

</details>


### [2] [Interacting Quantum Symmetric Exclusion Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28255)
*Denis Bernard,Friedrich Hübner,Stefano Scopa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文引入并求解了相互作用量子对称排斥过程（IQSEP），这是一个描述带电粒子在格点上随机量子跳跃的模型族，其跳跃幅度取决于邻近站点的占据状态。该模型在没有相互作用时退化为标准的量子简单对称排斥过程，表现出相干扩散输运；在相互作用为同量级时捕获非相干扩散输运及其涨落。通过适当缩放相互作用强度与格点间距，作者定义了一个介观标度区间，在连续热力学极限中保留有限的相干长度，为相互作用扩散系统中量子相干性的涨落提供标度理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准量子简单对称排斥过程仅能描述相干扩散输运，而宏观涨落理论（MFT）只能处理非相干扩散。需要一个统一的框架来桥接这两种行为，特别是刻画相互作用扩散系统中量子相干性在大尺度上的涨落，这超出了标准涨落流体动力学（fluctuating hydrodynamics）的范畴。

Method: 作者构造了IQSEP模型族，使跳跃幅度依赖于邻近站点占用情况。研究三种情况：(1)无相互作用时退化到标准量子SSEP（相干扩散）；(2)相互作用为O(1)时产生密度依赖的扩散系数和迁移率，与宏观涨落理论建立联系；(3)将相互作用强度按格点间距适当缩放，定义介观标度区间，使得在连续热力学极限下保留有限的相干长度，从而在相干与非相干行为之间插值。

Result: 该模型准确求解了从相干到非相干扩散输运的完整谱系；介观标度区间在连续极限下保留了有限相干长度；所建立的标度理论能够描述相互作用扩散系统中量子相干性的涨落，超越了标准涨落流体动力学的适用范围。

Conclusion: IQSEP提供了一个统一的解析框架，将量子相干输运、经典非相干扩散及其涨落纳入同一模型族中。介观标度机制为研究扩散系统中量子相干长度的涌现和大尺度相干性涨落提供了新的理论工具，补充了现有随机量子动力学的理论体系。

Abstract: We introduce and solve the Interacting Quantum Symmetric Exclusion Process (IQSEP), a family of models describing the stochastic quantum hopping of charged particles along the edges of a lattice, with hopping amplitudes that depend on the occupations of neighbouring sites. In the absence of interactions, they reduce to the standard quantum simple symmetric exclusion process, exhibiting coherent diffusive transport. For interactions of order one, they capture incoherent diffusive transport and its fluctuations, characterized by density-dependent diffusivity and mobility, making contact with the macroscopic fluctuation theory. By rescaling the interaction strength appropriately with the lattice mesh, we define a mesoscopic scaling regime that retains a finite coherence length in the continuous thermodynamic limit. This regime interpolates between coherent behavior at small length scales and incoherent behavior at large scales. The resulting scaling theory accounts for fluctuations of quantum coherences in interacting diffusive systems, going beyond the scope of standard fluctuating hydrodynamics.

</details>


### [3] [When trajectory-based bounds fail: information thermodynamics under noisy feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27299)
*Natalia Ruiz-Pino,Ludovic Bellon,Antonio Prados*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过实验和理论研究了欠阻尼反馈控制系统中非马尔可夫控制序列下的信息引擎性能，发现转移熵、不可用信息和马尔可夫互信息三种界在真实条件下均非普遍最优，且测量噪声会使依赖轨迹统计的信息度量迅速劣化，而基于瞬时相关的度量相对稳健，导致随着噪声增大马尔可夫互信息界在较大范围内变得比不可用信息界更紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息引擎利用反馈从热涨落中提取功，已有多种信息论界被提出，但在测量存在噪声且反馈时间相关的现实条件下，这些界的相对表现尚不清楚，因此需要系统比较以揭示其适用边界。

Method: 在具有马尔可夫测量但非马尔可夫控制序列的欠阻尼反馈控制系统中，结合实验测量与理论分析，比较转移熵界、不可用信息界和马尔可夫互信息界三种代表性能界在不同测量噪声水平下的表现。

Result: 三种界均非普遍最优；测量噪声对依赖详细轨迹统计的信息度量影响更大，而基于瞬时相关的度量更稳健；轨迹依赖的界随噪声快速恶化，出现交叉现象使马尔可夫互信息界在较宽噪声范围内比不可用信息界更紧。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了在现实条件下依赖详细轨迹统计的信息论描述存在普遍局限，为超越理想化反馈协议的信息热力学提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Information engines exploit feedback to extract work from thermal fluctuations, extending the second law of thermodynamics through information-theoretic bounds. While several such bounds have been proposed, their relative performance under realistic conditions---where measurements are noisy and feedback is temporally correlated---remains largely unclear. Here, we experimentally and theoretically investigate this problem in an underdamped feedback-controlled system with Markovian measurements but non-Markovian control sequences. We compare three representative bounds derived from transfer entropy, unavailable information, and Markovian mutual information, and find that none is universally optimal. Instead, measurement noise preferentially affects information measures that rely on detailed trajectory statistics, while leaving quantities based on instantaneous correlations comparatively robust. As a consequence, trajectory-dependent bounds deteriorate rapidly, giving rise to a crossover in which the Markovian mutual-information bound becomes tighter than the unavailable-information bound over a broad range of measurement noise. Our results reveal a general limitation of information-theoretic descriptions that rely on detailed trajectory statistics in realistic settings and provide a unified perspective on information thermodynamics beyond idealised feedback protocols.

</details>


### [4] [From pink to brown: Interaction-driven noise color shift in the Aubry-Andr{é} Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27340)
*M. Jiménez-Valdez,S. A. Montes-Camacho,E. J. Torres-Herrera*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视了一维准周期势格点中的金属-绝缘体相变，使用自旋1/2粒子及可调最近邻相互作用进行建模。研究发现，在无相互作用情况下，功率谱（连续能级间距的傅里叶变换模平方）无法清晰捕捉相变信号，始终呈现布朗噪声行为；而在有相互作用情况下，金属相表现出粉红噪声谱波动，绝缘相则表现为布朗噪声。能级间距分布、能级数方差以及能量本征态结构均可作为相变的可靠指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在寻找一种能够更可靠地表征一维准周期势系统中金属-绝缘体相变的光谱统计工具，特别是考察功率谱这一噪声分析工具在相互作用和无相互作用两种场景下对相变的区分能力，以弥补传统统计量可能存在的不足。

Method: 使用自旋1/2粒子模型，在含在位准周期势的一维晶格中加入可调最近邻相互作用。通过定义连续能级间距的傅里叶变换模平方（即功率谱）作为主要表征量，同时结合能级间距分布、能级数方差和能量本征态结构等统计量，对比分析有/无相互作用情况下金属相与绝缘相的不同光谱波动特征。

Result: 无相互作用时，功率谱在各相区间均呈现布朗噪声行为，无法给出清晰的相变信号；引入相互作用后，金属相呈现粉红噪声特征而绝缘相呈现布朗噪声特征，形成明显区分。能级间距分布与能级数方差进一步支持了这些发现，且能量本征态结构与统计量在两种场景下均能作为相变的可靠指标。

Conclusion: 功率谱作为一种光谱波动诊断工具，在相互作用存在时能够有效区分金属相与绝缘相，表现出粉红噪声与布朗噪声的显著差异；但在无相互作用体系中其分辨能力有限。综合多种统计量与本征态结构分析，可以在不同条件下提供对相变的可靠判定。

Abstract: We revisit the metal-insulator transition in a one-dimensional lattice subjected to an on-site quasiperiodic potential, modeled using spin-1/2 particles with tunable nearest-neighbor interactions. To characterize the transition, we examine the power-spectrum, defined as the squared modulus of the Fourier transform of consecutive level spacings. In the absence of interactions, the power-spectrum fails to capture clear signatures of the transition, consistently exhibiting brown-noise-like behavior across regimes. In contrast, the interacting case reveals a marked change: the metallic phase displays spectral fluctuations consistent with pink noise, while the insulating phase is characterized by brown noise. These results are supported by analyses of the level spacing distribution and the level number variance. Additionally, we demonstrate that both statistics and the structure of energy eigenstates provide reliable indicators of the transition, in both interacting and non-interacting scenarios.

</details>


### [5] [Non-reciprocity drives a Brownian dimer out of equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27740)
*Suman Pramanik,Soham Dutta,Arnab Saha*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文分析了由两个过阻尼单体组成的二维布朗二聚体最小模型，单体被困在各向同性谐振子势中，并通过违反牛顿作用-反作用原理的非互易谐振弹簧耦合。研究表明，仅非互易相互作用就能在无外部时变驱动且接触单一热浴的情况下驱动系统远离平衡态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非互易相互作用作为一种内禀机制，能否在无外部驱动和单一热浴条件下自主驱动系统远离热力学平衡，从而研究非平衡态的基本来源。

Method: 采用二维布朗二聚体模型，包含两个过阻尼单体和各向同性谐振子势阱，以非互易弹簧（违反牛顿第三定律）耦合。对零静止长度极限，精确解析求解稳态概率分布和概率流；对有限静止长度弹簧，采用数值方法计算。

Result: 证明非互易相互作用单独即可使系统远离平衡态。零静止长度弹簧极限下，模型精确映射到布朗回旋（Brownian gyration）这一非平衡现象；有限静止长度情形则以数值方式给出稳态分布和电流。

Conclusion: 非互易弹簧耦合是一种无需外部驱动即可产生非平衡稳态的机制，其零静止长度极限与布朗回旋现象建立联系，为理解活性物质等非平衡系统的能量注入机制提供了理论洞见。

Abstract: We consider the minimal model of a two dimensional Brownian dimer consisting of two overdamped monomers, trapped in an isotropic harmonic potential and mutually coupled by a non-reciprocal harmonic spring that violates Newton's action-reaction principle. We have shown that the non-reciprocal interaction alone can drive the system far from equilibrium, in the absence of any external time dependent drive and being in contact with a single thermal bath. The exact steady state probability distribution and current are explicitly calculated for the zero-rest-length limit of the spring, which eventually maps our model to another non-equilibrium phenomenon, called Brownian gyration. For a spring with finite rest length, these quantities are calculated numerically.

</details>


### [6] [Ensemble theory of thermodiffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27839)
*R. Belousov,J. Elliott,A. Erzberger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用路径系综理论构建图上的动力学模型，将热输运与扩散输运耦合表示。仅通过一次平衡模拟校准，即可精确预测硬核气体在广泛温度梯度下的非平衡质量分布，并可解析估计Soret系数等输运性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温度差驱动的热流会产生物质梯度（如硬核气体原子模拟中观察到的Soret效应）。需要一种理论框架能够统一描述耦合的热扩散与质量输运，并用少量数据实现精确预测。

Method: 使用路径系综理论（path-ensemble theory）将耦合的热输运和扩散输运表示为图上的动力学过程。模型仅从单一平衡模拟中校准参数，即可外推到非平衡条件下的质量分布预测。

Result: 该模型在广泛施加的温度梯度范围内准确预测了硬核气体的非平衡质量分布，并能通过解析表达式估计输运性质（如Soret系数）。

Conclusion: 基于路径系综的图动力学模型为预测非平衡热扩散耦合输运提供了一种高效且准确的框架，仅需一次平衡模拟校准即可捕获广泛的非平衡行为。

Abstract: Heat flow sustained by a temperature difference generates a gradient of matter as observed, for example, in atomistic simulations of hard-core gases. We use path-ensemble theory to represent the coupled thermal and diffusive transport by dynamics on a graph. Calibrated from a single equilibrium simulation, this model accurately predicts nonequilibrium mass distribution in a hard-core gas across a wide range of applied temperature gradients. It also enables estimation of transport properties, such as the Soret coefficient, using analytical expressions.

</details>


### [7] [Mpemba effect in a chemomechanical model of the Kinesin molecular motor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27998)
*Karthik Cheruvary,Arnab Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文首次在生物学相关的非平衡过程中研究Mpemba效应，以驱动蛋白分子马达的六态化学机械网络模型为对象，发现该系统在化学平衡态存在Mpemba效应，并表明打破细致平衡主要重塑Mpemba相图而非定性改变弛豫现象，且马达速度的弛豫也反映了潜在的异常弛豫动力学，为实验检测提供了可行途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mpemba效应已在广泛的物理系统中被大量研究，但其在生物学相关非平衡过程中的作用尚未被探索。本文动机在于填补这一空白，研究分子马达这类典型的生物非平衡系统中是否存在异常弛豫现象。

Method: 采用驱动蛋白分子马达的六态化学机械网络模型，分别在平衡态和非平衡条件下研究其异常弛豫；通过分析自由能景观理解平衡态Mpemba效应的定性特征；通过改变机械和化学驱动（打破细致平衡）探究非平衡条件对Mpemba相图的影响；并分析马达速度弛豫与底层随机动力学异常弛豫的对应关系。

Result: 确认了化学平衡态下Mpemba效应的存在；发现机械和化学驱动主要重塑Mpemba相图，但在物理相关参数范围内不改变弛豫现象的定性特征；马达速度的弛豫镜像了底层随机动力学的异常弛豫，提供了实验上可观测的Mpemba效应特征。

Conclusion: 分子马达可作为研究生命系统中异常弛豫的有前景的基准系统，本文为在非平衡生化网络中探索Mpemba效应提供了更广泛的框架。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect, wherein a system prepared farther from equilibrium relaxes faster than one initially closer to equilibrium, has been extensively investigated in a wide range of physical systems. In contrast, its role in biologically relevant non-equilibrium processes remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate anomalous relaxation in the six-state chemomechanical network model of the Kinesin molecular motor under both equilibrium and non-equilibrium conditions. We first establish the existence of the Mpemba effect in chemical equilibrium and show that many of its qualitative features can be understood from the underlying free-energy landscape. We then examine the effects of mechanical and chemical driving, showing that breaking detailed balance primarily reshapes the Mpemba phase diagram without qualitatively altering the relaxation phenomenology over the physically relevant parameter regime. Finally, we demonstrate that the relaxation of the motor velocity also mirrors the anomalous relaxation of the underlying stochastic dynamics, thereby identifying an experimentally accessible signature of the Mpemba effect. Our results establish molecular motors as a promising baseline for studying anomalous relaxation in living systems and suggest a broader framework for exploring the Mpemba effect in non-equilibrium biochemical networks.

</details>


### [8] [Three-Dimensional Kardar--Parisi--Zhang Scaling in Polariton Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28106)
*Junhui Cao,Denis Novokreschenov,Artem Alexandrov,Timothy Halpin-Healy,Alexey Kavokin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出三维激子-极化激元晶体作为观测3D KPZ普适性的平台，通过从随机驱动-耗散Gross-Pitaevskii方程出发消除密度和储层模式，得到有效的3+1维KPZ方程，数值模拟显示一阶相干函数的标度行为与3D KPZ基准指数β=0.1845、χ=0.3135一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPZ普适性是微观细致平衡破坏产生宏观标度行为的范例，但已在1D和2D中被探索，在三维空间维数中展示KPZ标度仍然是一个重大挑战。本文旨在提供可控的三维平台来观测3D KPZ普适性。

Method: 从三维光子晶体下极化激元能带中凝聚体的随机驱动-耗散Gross-Pitaevskii方程出发，消除大质量密度和储层模式，推导出有效的3+1维KPZ方程。对KPZ方程和完整驱动-耗散极化激元模型进行数值模拟。

Result: 数值模拟显示中间渐近区域中一阶相干性满足-ln|g(1)(0,Δt)|∝|Δt|^(2β)和-ln|g(1)(Δr,0)|∝|Δr|^(2χ)，其指数与3+1维KPZ基准值β=0.1845和χ=0.3135一致。

Conclusion: 三维极化激元晶体被确定为通向高维非平衡普适性的可控量子流体路径。

Abstract: Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) universality provides an example of macroscopic scaling generated by microscopic violation of detailed balance. While one- and two-dimensional realizations have been explored in driven condensates and growing interfaces, demonstrating KPZ scaling in three spatial dimensions remains a major challenge. Here we propose a three-dimensional exciton-polariton crystal as a platform for observation of 3D KPZ universality. Starting from a stochastic driven-dissipative Gross-Pitaevskii equation for a condensate formed in a three-dimensional photonic-crystal lower-polariton band, we eliminate the massive density and reservoir modes and obtain an effective $3+1$-dimensional KPZ equation for the condensate phase. We derive closed-form phase boundaries and show that the onset of oscillations is explicitly controlled by network heterogeneity through the degree moment ratio. Degree heterogeneity decouples the spiking rate per neuron m from the spiking rate per synapse u, generating physical phenomena absent in homogeneous systems. These include (i) kinematic waves of sequential, degree-ordered activations propagating from the network periphery to the hubs, and (ii) a low-temperature phase-separated state emerging via a pitchfork bifurcation. We prove that for highly heterogeneous topologies, this phase-separated fixed point stabilizes and dynamically destroys the synchronized limit cycle. These results provide a mathematical framework for understanding how heterogeneity regulates macroscopic oscillations and out-of-equilibrium transitions in neural networks

In one dimension, an attempted deposition is therefore rejected whenever the target site is an isolated vacancy. Despite the apparent simplicity of this rule, no exact analytical solution of the original model is currently known. We derive an infinite hierarchy of kinetic equations governing its dynamics and introduce a systematic closure scheme that can be implemented at arbitrary finite order. Solving the resulting systems order by order, we show that the corresponding kinetics converges rapidly toward the exact behavior. At high order, the method yields an exceptionally accurate estimate of the jamming density while retaining explicit analytical expressions for the full time-dependent evolution.

</details>

Abstract: The physics of quantum chaos and diffusive transport is typically studied in settings with microscopic disorder or many-body interactions. In this Letter, we demonstrate that these phenomena can arise purely from geometric randomness. By studying non-interacting quantum particles on random locally tree-like layered graphs with uniform couplings, we show that the geometric randomness and effective graph dimensionality dictates the presence of chaotic dynamics or lack thereof. These graphs can be considered as structurally disordered generalisations of regular square lattices or ladders, or equivalently as multi-component one-dimensional chains with random links between the components. We find that an extensive layer size yields robust quantum chaos, level repulsion, and diffusive transport. Conversely, in the quasi-one-dimensional limit, we find the coexistence of extensive number of localised and delocalised states -- this leads to suppressed level repulsion accompanied by the latter driving ballistic transport. These results establish geometric randomness as a fundamental and independent mechanism for generating and tuning quantum chaos.

</details>

Abstract: The compressible Euler--Riesz equations arise in the modelling of a wide range of physical phenomena, including stellar dynamics, plasma physics, and mathematical biology. We study rotating steady states of the attractive compressible Euler--Riesz equations, which we call rotating Riesz stars, and establish existence and nonlinear stability or instability results in both the mass-subcritical and mass-supercritical regimes. In the mass-subcritical regime, we prove the existence of rotating Riesz stars under suitable subhomogeneity assumptions on the angular momentum profile. We then establish their nonlinear stability through a concentration compactness argument adapted to the axisymmetric setting. Rotation creates new compactness difficulties, most notably the possibility that minimising sequences are tight along rings whose radii diverge to infinity. In the mass-supercritical regime, we prove existence in the polytropic setting under suitable superhomogeneity assumptions on the angular momentum profile, thereby extending the theory beyond the small angular velocity regime. The proof requires a careful analysis of mass-preserving scalings, which are more delicate than in the non-rotating case. Finally, by analysing the concavity of the free-energy along these scalings, we establish the instability of the resulting mass-supercritical rotating Riesz stars. Our results show that rotation can have either a stabilising or a destabilising effect, depending on the singularity of the Riesz interaction.

Abstract: This paper concerns a variational system of nonlinear elliptic equations that generalizes the classical Brezis-Nirenberg problem. In addition to considering vector-valued unknown functions in place of scalar-valued unknown functions, the problem under consideration generalizes the Brezis-Nirenberg problem in two directions. First, the nonlinearity is the sum of two homogeneous functions of suitable homogeneity degrees. Second, the Sobolev-critical term in the nonlinearity is nonlocal. We establish conditions under which a nontrivial solution to the system under consideration is guaranteed to exist and conditions under which the system under consideration admits only the trivial solution.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sirakov提出了等频双分量三次薛定谔系统的正解唯一性猜想，该问题在弱耦合范围内长期未解决。证明唯一性的关键在于排除分量归一化比率为非常数的径向解，这是长期以来的主要技术难点。

Result: 在N=2,3维和弱耦合范围0<β<μ1≤μ2内，该系统在H^1(R^N)×H^1(R^N)中恰好存在一个正解（模去同时平移），且该解是由标量方程-Δw+w=w^3的唯一正径向解构造的同步态。

Abstract: Let $N\in\{2,3\}$, $0<μ_1\leqμ_2$, and $0<β<μ_1$. We prove that the equal-frequency two-component cubic Schrödinger system \[
  -Δu+u=μ_1u^3+βuv^2,
  \qquad
  -Δv+v=μ_2v^3+βu^2v
  \quad\text{in }\mathbb{R}^N \] has exactly one positive solution in $H^1(\mathbb{R}^N)\times H^1(\mathbb{R}^N)$ modulo simultaneous translations. More precisely, every positive solution is a simultaneous translate of the synchronized state constructed from the unique positive radial solution of $-Δw+w=w^3$ in $\mathbb{R}^N$. This settles Sirakov's equal-frequency uniqueness conjecture throughout the weak-coupling range.

After normalization, the two components satisfy scalar equations with a common potential. We construct a weighted Pohozaev functional for the system together with a correction term and prove that both the corrected functional and the associated weighted functional are strictly positive. Combining these sign properties with a radial flux identity and an auxiliary quotient associated with the component ratio forces synchronization.

</details>

Abstract: We establish a dimension-efficient neural network approximation theory for solutions to fractional parabolic equations with lower-order drift and potential terms. Motivation: 该研究的动机源于广义相对论中的最优局域化问题（又称引力屏蔽问题）。为了控制Einstein约束方程解的渐近行为（见于配套论文arXiv:2312.17706），作者引入了谐波稳定性、径向稳定性和壳层稳定性的概念，需要在任意维度的球面上严格证明这些稳定性性质，从而完成由Carlotto和Schoen发起的引力屏蔽纲领。

Method: 本文通过加权Poincaré不等式、Korn不等式和Hardy不等式来证明三种稳定性性质（谐波、径向、壳层稳定性）。在证明过程中，引入了哈密顿与动量泛函（称为"壳层泛函"），并证明这些泛函具有单调性和半强制（semi-coercivity）性质。还系统地分析了任意维度下相关几何常数的行为，从而覆盖从任意小孔径胶接锥到整个球面的全部情形。

Result: 证明了稳定性条件对广泛一类局域化函数均成立：理论适用于任意小的局域化区域（对应任意小孔径的胶接锥），也适用于整个球面（即无局域化情形）。所引入的壳层泛函满足单调性和半强制性质，其结构暗示与其他曲率相关几何问题中泛函的潜在类比。这为任意维度中任意小孔径胶接锥情形下的引力屏蔽问题提供了完备的稳定性验证。

Conclusion: 本文通过不等式方法建立了球面上四阶与二阶微分算子（源于线性化Einstein约束算子）的谐波、径向和壳层稳定性，完成了任意维度下任意小孔径胶接锥的引力屏蔽纲领。壳层泛函的引入不仅服务于当前证明，还揭示了与其他几何问题可能存在的深层联系，为后续研究提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: We consider fourth-order and second-order partial differential operators localized on domains of the sphere in arbitrary dimension. These operators arise as weighted compositions of the linearized Einstein constraint operators and their adjoints, and played a key role in our resolution of the optimal localization problem in general relativity, also referred to as the gravitational shielding problem. To control the asymptotic behavior of solutions to Einstein's constraints in our companion paper (preprint arXiv:2312.17706), we introduced the notions of harmonic, radial, and shell stability. Harmonic stability controls the borderline harmonic modes, radial stability governs the radial evolution of spherical averages, and shell stability controls the coupled radial-angular evolution of solutions. In the present paper, we establish that these stability properties follow from weighted Poincaré, Korn, and Hardy inequalities. Furthermore, in arbitrary dimension, we investigate the behavior of the associated geometric constants, and conclude that the stability conditions hold for a broad class of localization functions; the theory applies to arbitrarily small localization domains, corresponding to gluing cones with arbitrarily small aperture. At the opposite extreme, our conditions also hold on the entire sphere, corresponding to the absence of localization. This completes, for gluing cones of arbitrarily small aperture in every dimension, the program initiated by A. Carlotto and R. Schoen on gravitational shielding and the construction of solutions enjoying super-harmonic decay estimates. In our proofs, we introduce Hamiltonian and momentum functionals, which we call shell functionals, and show that they enjoy monotonicity and semi-coercivity properties; their structure also suggests possible analogies with functionals arising in other curvature-related geometric problems.

</details>

Abstract: We present a framework combining discrete gradient (DG) methods with the Scalar Auxiliary Variable (SAV) approach to construct structure-preserving integrators for dissipative and conservative systems. The key observation is that SAV quadratization lifts the dynamics to an extended state space on which the modified energy has an exact discrete-gradient identity. This viewpoint yields three integrators with different accuracy and cost profiles: a first-order semi-implicit Forward Euler scheme, a second-order self-adjoint Midpoint scheme, and a second-order Predictive scheme with reduced implicitness. The construction extends to almost-Poisson systems and preserves selected Casimir invariants under an enforceable discrete condition. Numerical experiments cover the Allen--Cahn equation, an Ohta--Kawasaki-type nonlocal gradient flow, a double-well Hamiltonian oscillator, and a Poisson system with a nonlinear cubic Casimir.

See the PDF for the full abstract.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 该方法将TV去噪作为数值解的后处理步骤，通过减少节点值的总变差来抑制振荡。关键在于使用微分包含（differential inclusion）算法精确计算TV最小化问题的解，该算法无需调节参数，在一维中有限步收敛且保持总质量守恒。在二维情形下，算法按维度逐一应用一维算法。将该后处理方法作为限制器，用于Fourier伪谱方法和五阶有限差分格式。

Result: 该方法被用于求解标量守恒律、可压缩Euler方程和二维不可压Euler方程，取得了良好的数值效果。微分包含算法在一维中有限步计算TV最小化解，无需参数调节且保持总质量，二维通过逐维应用实现有效扩展。

Conclusion: 所提出的TV去噪后处理方法是一种通用且有效的振荡抑制手段，能够精确求解不满足子模性条件的一般TV最小化问题，并成功应用于多种守恒律方程和高阶数值格式。

Abstract: High-order numerical methods for conservation laws can generate spurious oscillations near discontinuities. We propose to suppress these oscillations by post-processing the numerical solution with a total variation (TV) denoising step that reduces the total variation of the nodal values. For a general analysis operator, the resulting discrete minimization problem need not satisfy the submodularity property that existing exact max-flow algorithms require, so we instead compute the TV minimizer exactly using a differential inclusion algorithm. In one dimension, the differential inclusion algorithm computes the TV minimizer in finitely many steps, requires no tuning of algorithmic parameters, and preserves the total mass. In two dimensions, we apply the 1D algorithm dimension by dimension. We test the method as a post-processing limiter for Fourier pseudospectral methods and a fifth-order finite difference scheme applied to scalar conservation laws, compressible Euler equations, and the two-dimensional incompressible Euler equations.

</details>

Abstract: We propose dynamical low-rank (DLR) type filters for data-assimilation problems based on stochastic differential equations (SDEs). In detail, first we derive a DLRA filter for minimizing jointly the mean and covariance error, as well as a strategy to efficiently include the relevant orthogonal directions. This last approach allows the main subspace to evolve also according to the observation operator. Those procedures naturally extend to a Kalman-Bucy type filter when dealing with linear drift, and to ensemble methods, too, resulting also suitable for problems described by nonlinear drift and possible non-Gaussian distribution. Moreover, we further propose a preliminary particle-type DLRA filter that shows potentiality in nonlinear settings. Numerical simulations show the efficacy of these procedures in relevant applications, opening up to further studies in these filtering directions.

Abstract: Computing numerical approximations of matrix functions frequently relies on the efficient evaluation of high-degree matrix polynomials. Abstract: Solving large, sparse linear systems is a core task in scientific computing, and efficient iterative solvers rely critically on effective and robust preconditioning. While classical methods such as algebraic multigrid (AMG) are highly scalable, their robustness can degrade on indefinite or nonsymmetric systems where heuristics originally developed for elliptic PDEs are less reliable. Recently, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as data-driven preconditioners; yet, the practical impact of imposing an AMG-style hierarchy remains underexplored for general sparse matrices. On a benchmark of over 800 sparse matrices, we compare against classical AMG, single-level ILUT, and state-of-the-art GNN preconditioners, and characterize the regimes where multilevel graph neural preconditioning improves convergence or, conversely, introduces overhead relative to strong single-level baselines. These results highlight both the promise and the limitations of enforcing AMG-style multilevel structure in learned preconditioners for large-scale scientific simulations.

We prove that $ε$-lc adjoint foliated surfaces with nef adjoint canonical divisor and a nef and big integral polarization form a bounded family, provided that the volume of their sum is bounded from above. As applications, we establish boundedness and effective birationality for adjoint foliated surfaces of general type, as well as a uniform positive lower bound for their volumes. Finally, for $ε$-lc Calabi--Yau adjoint foliated surfaces, we prove that the volumes of the canonical divisors of rank one foliations on the underlying surfaces belong to a fixed discrete set.

</details>

### [58] [Performance Benchmarking: Software for the Density Matrix Renormalization Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28369)
*Per Sehlstedt,Paolo Bientinesi,Lars Karlsson*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向性能的基准测试框架，用于系统比较密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）算法的多种软件实现，并通过量化八种实现的性能差异（最多可达两个数量级）揭示了参数配置和实现特性对性能的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMRG算法已有超过50种软件实现，但现有性能评估稀缺且缺乏统一标准，许多评估要么不够完整无法进行有意义的比较，要么侧重于性能直接比较之外的其他目标，导致对这些实现之间差异的理解有限。

Method: 提出一个面向性能的基准测试框架，将其应用于八种DMRG实现，量化其性能；同时考察多种参数设置、优化策略和实现特有功能，以展示参数配置如何影响性能以及系统评估如何揭示非显而易见的权衡。

Result: 发现不同实现之间在参数对齐时性能差异可达两个数量级；同一实现内部在不同参数配置下性能差异同样显著。这些结果表明严格的性能评估能带来重要洞察价值。

Conclusion: 该框架和结果可作为更严格基准测试的起点，帮助用户和开发者做出知情决策，并支持未来开发更高效软件的工作。

Abstract: The performance of scientific software often determines the scale of problems that can be solved in practice. As multiple implementations of the same algorithm emerge, systematic evaluation is needed to compare their strengths and limitations. The density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) algorithm, widely used to study quantum systems, has over 50 software implementations. These implementations vary in multiple aspects that can strongly affect performance. However, despite the need, performance evaluations of these implementations are scarce and lack a consistent standard; many existing evaluations are either too incomplete to enable meaningful comparisons or focus on objectives other than direct performance comparisons, thereby limiting understanding of how the implementations compare. Here, we present a performance-oriented benchmarking framework to facilitate meaningful comparisons of DMRG implementations, and we apply it to quantify the performance of eight implementations, highlighting similarities and differences among them. Furthermore, we examine multiple parameter settings, optimization strategies, and implementation-specific features to demonstrate how parameter configuration can affect performance and how systematic evaluation can reveal non-obvious trade-offs. The results show significant performance differences, up to two orders of magnitude in some cases, not only between different implementations when aligning parameters, but also within the same implementation when comparing different parameter configurations. Hence, our results demonstrate the significant value and insight that can be gained from conducting rigorous performance evaluations. Using our results and framework as a starting point, more rigorous benchmarking will ultimately help users and developers make informed decisions and support future development efforts to build better, more efficient software.

</details>


### [59] [Fast and Accurate Foundation Models for Equivariant Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28461)
*Seán R. Kavanagh,Chuin Wei Tan,Menghang Wang,Marc L. Descoteaux,Gabriel de Miranda Nascimento,Ulrik Unneberg,Laura Zichi,Francesco Libbi,Norma Rivano,Austin Glover,Vivek Bharadwaj,Anders Johansson,William C. Witt,Albert Musaelian,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了等变机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）作为基础模型在追求高推理速度、强可扩展性和高精度之间的权衡。作者基于NequIP和Allegro等变架构提出了一系列基础势能模型，在社区基准测试中（涵盖材料发现、热导率预测、力学和热力学性质）展现出领先的推理速度和优异的精度。此外，NequIP基础设施中的加速实现使得在超大数据集上以显著降低的计算成本训练高精度基础势能成为可能。研究还表明，改进材料发现模型的精度应着重于数据集多样性以及改进过渡金属化合物能量表面的描述一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变MLIPs作为基础模型在下游微调中广泛应用，但其推理速度、训练速度和精度之间存在竞争性权衡。尤其是在超大数据集场景下，数据效率不再是最关键的，如何在这种情境下平衡速度和精度成为一个核心挑战。

Method: 作者系统地检验了等变MLIPs在极大数据集场景下同时实现高推理/训练速度和精度的可能性，基于NequIP和Allegro等变架构构建了一系列基础势能模型，并在NequIP基础设施中实施了多种加速策略以实现大规模训练。

Result: 所提出的模型在多个社区基准测试中展现出领先的推理速度和强可扩展性，同时保持优异的精度，覆盖材料发现、热导率预测以及近平衡力学和热力学性质等领域。NequIP的加速实现显著降低了超大数据集上训练高精度基础势能的计算成本。

Conclusion: 等变MLIPs能够在大型数据集场景下有效兼顾速度与精度；进一步提升材料发现模型精度的关键方向在于增强数据集多样性和改善过渡金属化合物能量表面描述的一致性，而非在等变架构精度层面的单点改进。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have emerged as a transformative tool for computational materials science and chemistry, with universal potentials trained on large and diverse datasets now routinely deployed as 'foundation models' for downstream fine-tuning in targeted chemical spaces. Many scientific applications of the resulting models, such as molecular dynamics (MD), require high inference and training speeds as well as accuracy. In this work, we examine the limits of equivariant MLIPs, which directly encode physical symmetries in model architectures, to achieve these competing targets -- particularly in the regime of extremely large datasets where data efficiency is less critical. We show how this trade-off can be addressed, and present a family of foundation potentials in the NequIP and Allegro equivariant MLIP architectures which achieve leading inference speeds and strong scalability as well as excellent accuracies across a range of community benchmarks -- spanning materials discovery, thermal conductivity prediction, and near-equilibrium mechanical and thermodynamic properties. Accelerations implemented within the NequIP infrastructure now permit training of high-accuracy foundation potentials on ultra-large datasets with dramatically reduced computational cost. Alongside, we show that efforts to improve model accuracy for materials discovery should focus on dataset diversity and improved, consistent descriptions of transition metal compound energy surfaces.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [60] [Recursive transformers for semiconductor thermo-mechanical reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27251)
*Kart-leong Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了三种递归Transformer架构（微型递归模型、深度递归Transformer和简单递归Transformer），用于替代昂贵的工程设计中基于第一性原理的仿真。在低维小数据集工程场景下，递归权值共享Transformer相比传统Transformer能更好地平衡预测精度、参数效率与计算成本，并在半导体封装热机械可靠性分析和Laplace PDE电容场求解两个任务上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer在小规模、低维工程数据集上严重过参数化，导致过拟合而非精度提升，同时带来不必要的内存和计算开销。需要转向"增加计算而非增加参数"的架构，以适应工程设计中仿真数据昂贵、数据量有限的现实约束。

Method: 提出一种硬件感知的递归Transformer范式评估框架，系统比较三种递归架构：Tiny Recursive Model、新提出的Depth Recursive Transformer以及简单的递归Transformer。在性能指标上综合考量预测精度（Recall、MRR）、参数量和计算复杂度（FLOPs），并在两个低维工程任务（先进封装热机械可靠性分析、Laplace PDE迭代数值求解）上验证。

Result: 三种递归Transformer在两项低维工程预测任务上的对比结果表明，递归权值共享Transformer在预测精度、参数效率和计算成本之间提供了有效且可推广的权衡，优于过参数化的传统Transformer架构。

Conclusion: 在资源受限工程场景下，递归权值共享Transformer是整个可行的替代方案，能有效缓解小数据下过拟合问题，并为工程代理建模提供了实用的架构选型设计指南。

Abstract: Transformer-based surrogate models are increasingly used to replace expensive first-principles simulation in engineering design. But conventional transformer architectures are often over parameterized for the small, low-dimensional datasets typical of engineering design spaces, where large simulation data is expensive to generate. Under these conditions, excess parameter capacity leads to overfitting rather than improved accuracy, while also incurring unnecessary memory and compute overhead. This motivates a shift towards architectures that focus on additional compute rather than additional learnable parameters. This paper presents a hardware-aware evaluation of three recursive transformer paradigms for surrogate thermo-mechanical analysis of advanced packages: a)Tiny Recursive Model, b) our proposed Depth Recursive transformer, c) and a simple recursive transformer. We systematically compare their predictive performance (Recall, Mean Reciprocal Rank), parameter count, computational complexity (FLOPs), providing practical design guidelines for selecting recursive transformer architectures under resource-constrained scenarios. We validate this principle on two low-dimensional engineering prediction tasks: 1) thermo-mechanical reliability analysis of advanced semiconductor packages, where stress and warpage from thermal cycling must be evaluated repeatedly across a design-of-experiments sweep under costly finite element analysis (FEA). 2) Laplace PDE iterative numerical solver for capacitance field. Overall, recursive weight-sharing transformers provide an effective and generalizable trade-off between prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency, and computational cost for small data engineering surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [61] [Regularizing modality contribution drift in multimodal continual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27260)
*Zhen Zhang,Jielei Chu,Bin Liu,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模态贡献漂移（MCD）概念及其度量指标，并设计持续模态贡献漂移正则化方法（CMCDR），通过保留旧任务中模态贡献结构来缓解多模态持续学习中的灾难性遗忘，涵盖基于回放和无回放两种版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMCL方法主要关注跨模态表示对齐或语义相似度，但忽视了个体模态的相对贡献及其交互作用在增量任务间是否保持稳定。这种决策层面的漂移（称为模态贡献漂移MCD）是导致持续学习性能下降的关键因素，而现有方法无法可靠地缓解这一漂移。

Method: 作者首先提出MCD分数，结合贡献强度与相对依赖性的变化来衡量模态贡献漂移（通过对模态子集的受控干预实现）。在此基础上，提出CMCDR框架，包含两个版本：基于回放版本利用模态子集干预作为诊断探针，对比当前模型与冻结旧模型在存储样本上的贡献轮廓；无回放版本则以当前任务样本为探针，蒸馏冻结模型对旧任务的贡献响应，无需样本即可正则化贡献轮廓。

Result: 在多模态类增量学习和持续视觉问答任务上的实验验证了CMCDR的通用性和有效性，证明其能够有效缓解模态贡献漂移并提升持续学习性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式约束旧任务中模态贡献结构的稳定性，CMCDR能够更可靠地缓解MMCL中的遗忘问题，且在有无旧样本回放的场景下均适用，为多模态持续学习提供了新的正则化视角。

Abstract: Multimodal continual learning (MMCL) aims to learn emerging knowledge from multimodal data while preserving knowledge. To mitigate forgetting, current MMCL methods usually focus on cross-modal representation alignment or semantic similarity, but they overlook whether the relative contributions of individual modalities and their interactions remain stable across incremental tasks. We term this decision-level shift Modality Contribution Drift (MCD) and quantify it with the MCD score, which combines contribution-strength and relative-reliance changes under controlled interventions on modality subsets. Theoretical and empirical analyses further explain why current MMCL methods cannot reliably mitigate this drift. To this end, we propose Continual Modality Contribution Drift Regularization (CMCDR), which preserves the modality contribution structure of previously learned tasks. Since MMCL settings differ in whether old exemplars are available, CMCDR includes both replay-based and replay-free versions. The replay-based version uses modality-subset interventions as diagnostic probes on stored old samples, compares their contribution profiles between the current model and a frozen previous model, and constrains changes in old-sample modality-specific and interaction contributions. The replay-free version uses current-task samples as probes and distills the frozen model's old-task contribution responses, thereby regularizing the observed contribution profile without exemplars. Experiments on multimodal class-incremental learning and continual visual question answering validate the generality and effectiveness of CMCDR.

</details>


### [62] [DoTime: A Synthetic Benchmark Generator for Interventional and Counterfactual Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27263)
*Dennis Thumm,Billy Tim Anthony,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DoTime——一个开放、可扩展、理论严谨的多元时序结构因果模型（TSCM）生成器，支持连续时间干预窗口、反事实采样、机制切换SCM等新能力，并发布为dotime PyPI包及四套冻结评估套件。实验表明，以DoTime作为先验的因果基础模型（PFN），其干预训练在方向预测精度上全面优于同容量的纯观测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列因果推断基准大多局限于观测数据、规模小或领域特定，在医疗、政策评估和气候科学等关键场景中，干预与反事实估计缺乏充分支撑。需要一种通用、可扩展、具有理论依据的数据生成器来系统评估和推动时序因果推断方法的发展。

Method: DoTime构建了多元时序结构因果模型生成器，包含四类关键新能力：连续时间干预窗口、带正定性守护的反事实采样、作为中断时间序列严格泛化的机制切换SCM、以及结构参数切换带来的非平稳动态和将趋势/结构断点置于评估窗内的确定性斜坡与正弦干预剖面。同时提供了10万条轨迹的训练规模快照、八种命名识别结构、共享噪声反事实等精确真值，并配套参考基线与评估框架。

Result: 在结构匹配的留出场景评估中，基于干预先验拟合的PFN在不同结构、轨迹长度和训练种子下，其方向预测准确率优势始终为正，即干预训练带来可测量的方向精度增益，显著优于同容量的观测模型。

Conclusion: DoTime填补了时序因果推断中干预与反事实估计基准缺失的空白，不仅提供了理论扎实、功能超越现有生成器的数据生成工具，还验证了干预预训练对因果基础模型性能的可度量提升，为后续研究奠定了可复现的评估基础。

Abstract: Most benchmarks for causal inference over time series are observational, small, or domain-specific, leaving interventional and counterfactual estimation under-served exactly where it matters most, such as in healthcare, policy evaluation, and climate science. We introduce \textbf{DoTime}, an open, scalable, and theoretically grounded generator of multivariate temporal structural causal models (TSCMs) with interventions, released as the \code{dotime} PyPI package together with four frozen evaluation suites. Beyond existing work, it adds capabilities absent from prior generators: continuous-time intervention \emph{windows}, counterfactual sampling modes with a positivity guard, regime-switching SCMs as a strict generalization of interrupted time series, non-stationary dynamics by construction with switching SCM parameters, and deterministic ramp and sinusoidal intervention profiles that place trends and structural breaks \emph{inside} the evaluation window. Moreover, it demonstrates the suitability of the generator as a prior for a causal foundation model reference implementation. The released suites span a training-scale snapshot of $100{,}000$ trajectories and eight named identification structures, each with exact ground truth: paired interventional trajectories from the same SCM throughout, and shared-noise counterfactuals in the continuous-time suite. We ship reference baseline implementations with an evaluation harness, and pose a falsifiable claim: interventional training buys a measurable direction-accuracy advantage over an observational model of identical capacity. It is tested across three training seeds per arm. Under structure-matched evaluation on held-out episodes, the interventional prior-fitted network's (PFN) gap is positive in every structure, trajectory length, and seed tested.

</details>


### [63] [PlatformBid: An Auto-Bidding Benchmark from a Unified Advertising Platform's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27265)
*Shengtian Yang,Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Zhiyi Lyu,Bo An,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PlatformBid，这是首个从统一广告平台视角设计的自动出价基准测试，涵盖同质竞争、异质竞争和促销竞争三种真实场景，并提出了基于流匹配的新方法BidFlow。在快手平台的在线实验中，BidFlow实现目标成本提升0.68%，验证了离线与在线的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动出价算法仅从DSP（需求方平台）视角出发，以最大化广告主转化率为目标，忽略了大型广告平台（如社交媒体和电商公司）已将SSP、DSP和Ad Exchange功能内部整合的现实。从平台整体视角出发，自动出价算法不仅要最大化广告主的转化，还要最大化平台的总收入。目前缺乏平台中心化的评估框架，这是推动自动出价研究前进的关键瓶颈。

Method: PlatformBid定义了三种反映真实自动出价场景的设置：(1)同质竞争——广告主使用相同算法；(2)异质竞争——广告主使用多样化算法策略；(3)促销竞争——部分广告主在大促期间（如黑色星期五）增加预算以促进销售。此外，作者提出了基于流匹配（flow-matching）的新型自动出价方法BidFlow，利用流匹配方法的表达能力强的策略表征来有效处理动态竞争环境。

Result: 系统评估了涵盖经典控制方法、基于强化学习的方法和最新生成式方法在内的多种现有自动出价算法。在快手平台上的在线实验表明，BidFlow在目标成本上实现了+0.68%的提升，为PlatformBid的离线-在线一致性提供了部署证据。

Conclusion: PlatformBid填补了平台中心化自动出价评估框架的空白，为自动出价研究提供了更贴近真实场景的基准。BidFlow证明了流匹配方法在动态竞争环境中的有效性，且在线实验结果验证了基准的可靠性和实际部署价值。

Abstract: Real-time bidding is central to computational advertising, comprising three elements: Supply Side Platform (SSP) selling ad impressions, Demand Side Platform (DSP) bidding for advertisers, and Ad Exchange conducting auctions between them. Traditional auto-bidding algorithms focus solely on the DSP side, maximizing advertiser conversions by adjusting bids against competitors. However, current big ad platforms, such as social media and e-commerce companies, now integrate SSP, DSP, and Ad Exchange functions internally. From such ad platforms' perspective, the goal of the auto-bidding algorithms is not only to maximize the advertisers' conversions, but also the total revenue of the platform. Given the lack of platform-centric evaluation frameworks and the pressing need to advance auto-bidding research, we propose PlatformBid - the first comprehensive benchmark designed from a unified ad platform's perspective. To accurately reflect the real-world auto-bidding scenarios, we define three representative settings: (1) homogeneous competition with identical algorithms across advertisers, (2) heterogeneous competition with diverse algorithmic strategies, and (3) promotional competition where some advertisers surge budgets for boosting sales during promotional events like Black Friday. We systematically evaluate a broad spectrum of existing auto-bidding methods across these settings, encompassing classical control methods, RL-based methods, and recent generative methods. Besides these methods, we further propose a novel auto-bidding method based on flow-matching, termed BidFlow, which leverages the flow-matching method's expressive policy representation to effectively handle dynamic competitive environments. Online experiments on Kuaishou further show a +0.68\% improvement in target cost, providing deployment evidence for the offline-online consistency of PlatformBid.

</details>


### [64] [Beyond KV Reconstruction: Functional Reconstruction for MLA Draft Models in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27269)
*Weiye Shi,Fanxu Meng,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的"功能重构"（Functional Reconstruction）方法，在MHA/GQA到MLA的转换后对每个注意力模块进行优化，使其在隐状态上复现原模型的输出投影响应，从而恢复被破坏的草稿-目标一致性，提升推测解码的接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLA因其紧凑的潜在状态替代增长的KV缓存而日益重要，但现有开放检查点多使用MHA/GQA，需要转换才能获得MLA的缓存效率。然而直接转换会引入注意力函数误差，尤其是低秩分解和RoPE处理导致的误差，这些误差在独立生成中尚可容忍，但会显著降低推测解码中草稿token的接受率，从而削弱推测解码的加速效果。

Method: 将MLA草稿构建视为功能重构而非缓存压缩问题。提出端到端（E2E）方法，对每个转换后的MLA注意力模块进行优化，使其在校准隐状态上复现原始MHA/GQA对应模块经过输出投影后的响应。该方法与具体转换器无关，作为转换后的后处理步骤，保留转换后的缓存和推理图，且不需要验证者logits或验证者监督。

Result: 在192个模型-转换器-后端-方法-任务配置上评估，覆盖4对Llama/Qwen草稿-目标对、TransMLA和MHA2MLA两种转换器、HF和vLLM两个后端，以及4个200提示词任务。以0.5个百分点为报告容差，功能重构在64个匹配任务单元中显著改善37个、基本不变26个、显著降低仅1个。

Conclusion: 功能重构方法能在大幅提升转换后MLA在推测解码中的草稿接受率，几乎不带来任何性能下降，是一种通用且有效的MHA/GQA-to-MLA转换后优化方法。代码和评估工件已开源。

Abstract: Multi-head latent attention (MLA) is increasingly important for long-context LLM inference because compact latent states replace the growing key-value (KV) cache and reduce decoding memory traffic. Yet most capable open checkpoints use multi-head or grouped-query attention (MHA/GQA), so conversion is needed to obtain MLA's cache efficiency without retraining from scratch. Speculative decoding offers complementary acceleration, but its speedup depends on agreement between draft proposals and target verification. We find that direct MHA/GQA-to-MLA conversion can sharply reduce this agreement: low-rank factorization and RoPE handling introduce attention-function errors that may be tolerable for standalone generation but substantially lower draft-token acceptance. We therefore formulate MLA draft construction as functional reconstruction rather than cache compression. Our end-to-end (E2E) method optimizes each converted MLA attention module to reproduce the post-output-projection response of its original MHA/GQA counterpart on calibration hidden states. This converter-agnostic post-conversion procedure preserves the converted cache and inference graph and requires neither verifier logits nor verifier supervision. We evaluate 192 model-converter-backend-method-task configurations spanning four Llama/Qwen draft-target pairs, TransMLA and MHA2MLA, HF and vLLM, and four 200-prompt tasks. With a 0.5-percentage-point reporting tolerance, Functional Reconstruction materially improves acceptance in 37 of 64 matched task cells, leaves 26 practically unchanged, and materially decreases one. Code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/swyhahaha/FunctionalMLA.

</details>


### [65] [RLPF: Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27271)
*Huihao Jing,Haozhe Cui,Wenbin Hu,Shaojin Chen,Haochen Shi,Changxuan Fan,Yuxuan Liu,Hanyu Yang,Sirui Zhang,Ziyi Chen,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLPF（从性能反馈中强化学习），将执行结果转化为分阶段奖励，用于训练代码智能体偏好更快的正确实现，而非仅将效率作为评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码模型越来越多地用执行反馈进行训练，但大多数训练信号仍止步于正确性。对于系统代码，两个程序可能通过相同测试但在运行时上差异巨大。然而运行时作为奖励是脆弱的：它只在程序正确后才有意义，且在不同任务间变化大，当大多数样本程序无法编译或运行时几乎无法提供指导。

Method: RLPF将执行结果转化为分阶段奖励：失败程序按执行进度排序，正确程序按从基线到专家参考的相对改进进行排名。这实现了在正确性之前的有效反馈以及正确性之后的性能敏感反馈。

Result: 使用RLPF在PerfCodeBench上微调Qwen3-32B，将正确且可运行的解决方案从11.1%提高到54.6%，相对效率从8.1%提高到38.6%。训练后的模型与更强的开源系统竞争，优化行为适度迁移到EffiBench-X。模型生成的参考提供有用但较弱的监督，完整组合奖励比仅正确性或仅运行时基线更可靠。

Conclusion: 代码智能体不仅可以被训练通过测试，还可以被训练优化它们编写的程序，这为代码生成模型的训练目标提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Code models are increasingly trained with execution feedback, but most training signals still stop at correctness. This leaves an important gap for systems code: two programs can pass the same tests while differing greatly in runtime. We study how to train code agents to prefer faster correct implementations, rather than treating efficiency only as an evaluation metric. The key difficulty is that runtime is a fragile reward. It is meaningful only after a program is correct, varies across tasks, and gives little guidance when most sampled programs fail to compile or run. We propose \textbf{RLPF}, reinforcement learning from performance feedback, which turns execution outcomes into a staged reward. Failed programs are ordered by execution progress, while correct programs are ranked by their relative improvement from the baseline toward the expert reference. This gives useful feedback before correctness and performance-sensitive feedback after correctness. Fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with RLPF on PerfCodeBench raises correct-and-runnable solutions from $11.1\%$ to $54.6\%$ and improves relative efficiency from $8.1\%$ to $38.6\%$. The trained model becomes competitive with stronger open-weight systems, and its optimization behavior transfers modestly to EffiBench-X. Additional studies show that model-generated references provide useful but weaker supervision, and that the full composite reward is more reliable than correctness-only or runtime-only baselines. These results suggest that code agents can be trained not only to pass tests, but also to optimize the programs they write.

</details>


### [66] [SDO: Structure-Aware Data Organization for Efficient LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27273)
*Jinliang Gao,Ning Yang,Hai Wang,Baili Xiao,Pin Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SDO（结构感知数据组织）框架，通过暴露驱动反馈机制动态调整小批量组成和样本暴露频率，基于表示空间结构逐轮组织数据，替代静态的数据分组预处理，在SFT、DPO和GRPO中加速收敛并提升各类问题上的精度均衡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的后训练成本高昂，现有效率优化主要关注样本选择或训练调度，但数据组织本身被视为静态预处理步骤。基于嵌入的分组方法在训练前构建固定分区，无法适应优化过程中样本暴露的演变，导致所有样本暴露相似，一些样本被冗余更新而另一些优化不足。

Method: SDO是即插即用的数据组织框架，具有暴露驱动的反馈机制。逐epoch操作于冻结的外部嵌入上，避免模型热身训练开销：(1) epoch内，通过KNN邻域遍历形成连贯小批量的局部感知批处理；(2) epoch间，暴露平衡调度记录每个样本的参与次数，降低过度暴露样本的采样概率以保持长期覆盖。

Result: 在SFT、DPO和GRPO上，SDO加速了收敛，增益在早中期阶段最大，产生更连贯的梯度，并在不同问题类型间实现更均衡的准确率，且不会永久排除训练样本。

Conclusion: SDO通过动态、结构感知的数据组织取代静态分组，在无额外模型热身开销的情况下改善后训练的效率与效果均衡性，是一种即插即用的有效方案。

Abstract: Post-training of large language models is expensive, and existing efficiency improvements mainly focus on selecting informative samples or designing training schedules. However, data organization itself is usually treated as a static preprocessing step: embedding-based grouping methods construct fixed partitions before training and cannot adapt to the evolving sample exposure during optimization. As a result, all samples receive similar exposure despite their different optimization needs, leading to redundant updates for some samples while leaving others under-optimized. To address this problem, we propose SDO (Structure-Aware Data Organization), a plug-and-play data organization framework with an exposure-driven feedback mechanism that organizes mini-batch composition and sample exposure according to representation-space structure. SDO operates epoch by epoch on frozen external embeddings, avoiding model warm-up training overhead: within each epoch, locality-aware batching forms coherent mini-batches via KNN neighborhood traversal; across epochs, exposure-balanced scheduling records per-sample participation and reduces the sampling probability of over-exposed samples to preserve long-term coverage. Across SFT, DPO, and GRPO, SDO accelerates convergence, with the largest gains observed in the early-to-mid phase, producing more coherent gradients and more balanced accuracy across question types without permanently excluding training samples.

</details>


### [67] [Rethinking EEG-Based Disease Diagnosis: Decoupling Instance Representation Learning from Subject-Level Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27274)
*Zhiyuan Ma,Zeyuan Li,Zhiyi Lu,Jiacheng Hao,Youlang Du,Zhen Jiang,Xinche Zhang,Yuhao Sun,Sen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BridgeMIL，一种两阶段框架，将实例表示学习与主体级监督解耦，用于基于EEG的疾病诊断。阶段1通过时间邻近窗口和子袋对齐进行无标签预训练，阶段2将编码器迁移至基于注意力的MIL聚合器并施加主体级监督。在3个EEG疾病数据集和5个骨干网络上，BridgeMIL在14/15项设置中取得最高平均准确率，整体平均准确率76.57%，比最强基线高出4.28个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG疾病诊断流程将记录切分为多个实例并继承主体标签，假设所有实例携带同等可靠的诊断证据，这与实际不符。尽管多实例学习(MIL)可避免继承标签问题，但EEG数据集主体数量远少于实例数量，限制了端到端MIL所学习表示的质量。因此需要一种既能利用丰富实例信息又不依赖实例级标签的方法。

Method: 提出BridgeMIL两阶段框架。阶段1预训练编码器，通过对齐时间上邻近的窗口和独立采样的主体内子袋来学习不变表示，采用方差和协方差正则化防止表征坍塌和冗余，且无需负样本对。阶段2将预训练编码器迁移到基于注意力的MIL聚合器，仅对主体预测施加监督，并通过特征保持机制限制表示漂移。

Result: 在3个EEG疾病数据集和5种骨干网络上，BridgeMIL在15种数据集-骨干组合中的14种取得最高平均准确率，整体平均准确率76.57%，较最强基线提高4.28个百分点。分析还发现实例间继承标签可靠性存在显著差异，性能对主体稀缺比对实例稀缺更敏感，且学习到的表征空间更具结构性，形成清晰的主体级聚类和更好的诊断类别分离。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了在利用丰富的EEG实例学习时，监督信号应与主体级预测目标对齐，而不应给单个实例赋予疾病标签，这有助于应对EEG疾病诊断中主体样本稀缺的挑战。

Abstract: EEG-based disease diagnosis requires one prediction per subject, yet common pipelines segment recordings into short instances, inherit the subject label for every instance, and train instance-level classifiers. This assumes that all instances provide equally reliable diagnostic evidence. Multiple instance learning (MIL) avoids inherited labels by treating each subject as a bag. However, EEG datasets contain far fewer subjects than instances, which can limit the quality of the representations learned by end-to-end MIL. We propose BridgeMIL, a two-stage framework that decouples instance representation learning from subject-level supervision. Stage 1 pretrains the encoder without inherited instance labels by aligning temporally nearby windows and independently sampled within-subject sub-bags. Variance and covariance regularization prevent collapse and reduce redundancy without negative pairs. Stage 2 transfers the encoder to an attention-based MIL aggregator, applies supervision only to subject predictions, and limits representation drift through feature retention. Across three EEG disease datasets and five representative backbones, BridgeMIL attains the highest mean accuracy in 14 of 15 dataset-backbone settings and an overall mean accuracy of 76.57%, 4.28 percentage points higher than the strongest baseline. Further analyses reveal substantial variation in inherited-label reliability across instances, greater performance sensitivity to subject scarcity than to instance scarcity, and a more structured representation space with distinct subject-wise clusters and improved separation between diagnostic classes. Together, these findings underscore the importance of aligning supervision with the subject-level prediction objective while learning from abundant EEG instances without assigning disease labels to individual instances.

</details>


### [68] [Flat Score, Amplified Failures: How the Error Budget Masks Damage in Quantized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27275)
*Jiwon Jang,Kisu Yang,Heuiseok Lim,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑"4-bit权重量化几乎无损"这一广泛说法在多轮工具调用智能体场景下的有效性。通过在τ²-bench上对两个开源模型家族（稠密和MoE变体）、两个领域、三种精度（16/8/4-bit）共八个单元格、每格456个episode进行全面测试，发现：标准指标上量化看似"免费"（无显著分数变化），但过程分析揭示量化将模型在全精度下已有的失败（如电信领域的工具名幻觉）放大至2.5倍（每任务+17.6分），且不产生新失败。分数持平的原因是基准的十错误预算吸收了额外失败；将预算缩减至两个错误时会重新暴露17分的差距，且仅在量化增加错误量的单元格出现。定向错误修复提示能精准消除损伤，证实了"掩蔽"假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献普遍报道4-bit权重量化几乎无损，但这些结论主要基于标准评估指标。作者质疑该说法在多轮工具调用智能体这种当前最关键的场景中是否成立——因为简单的任务分数可能掩盖量化带来的过程损伤，需要从过程层面深入检验量化对智能体失败模式的实际影响。

Method: 在τ²-bench基准上，对两个开源模型家族的稠密和MoE变体、两个领域（电信和零售）进行系统性测试，覆盖16/8/4-bit三种权重精度、八个单元格、每格456个episode。采用多重比较校正的显著性检验和等价性检验评估分数变化；通过过程分析（失败类型、错误量、失败集重叠度）刻画量化影响；引入两个诊断指标：缩减错误预算（从10到2）和定向错误修复提示，验证"掩蔽"假说。

Result: 标准指标上量化看似无损（无单元格分数变化通过多重比较校正，最大的过程损伤单元格中等价性检验将变化限定在±7.5分内）。但过程分析显示量化将已有的工具名幻觉放大至2.5倍（+17.6分/任务），几乎不产生新失败（失败集精度间秩相关≥0.94，仅0.18%新事件）。十错误预算吸收了额外失败导致分数持平；缩减到两错误时重新暴露17分差距，且仅在量化增加错误量的单元格出现。定向修复提示只在损伤存在的精度和领域精准移除损伤。

Conclusion: 4-bit量化对多轮工具调用智能体并非真正无损——标准分数掩盖了量化对已有失败模式的放大效应。作者建议在评估时除了报告任务奖励，还应同时报告两个来自现有日志的诊断指标：每通道错误率和缩减预算下的成功率，以更全面地反映量化等压缩技术的真实影响。

Abstract: Post-training quantization to 4-bit weights is widely reported to be nearly lossless. We test this claim for multi-turn, tool-calling agents, where it now matters most. On $τ^2$-bench, across two open-weight model families in dense and MoE variants and two domains (eight cells, 456 episodes each, at 16-, 8-, and 4-bit weights), quantization indeed looks free on the standard metric. No cell shows a score change that survives multiple-comparison correction, and in the cell that carries the largest process damage, equivalence testing bounds the change within $\pm$7.5 points. The process tells a different story. Quantization amplifies the failure the model already exhibits at full precision (tool-name hallucination in telecom, with the same directional trend in retail entity errors) by up to 2.5$\times$ in volume (+17.6 points per task), while creating essentially no new failures. The failure set is the same at every precision (rank correlation $\geq$ 0.94, 0.18% novel events). The score stays flat because the benchmark's ten-error budget absorbs the extra failures. Shrinking the budget to two errors re-exposes a score gap of 17 points, and it does so only in the one cell where quantization added error volume, exactly as the masking account predicts. A targeted error-repair prompt, run for five telecom models at every precision, removes the damage exactly and only where it lives. Both diagnostics, the per-channel error rate and success under a shrinking budget, come from logs benchmarks already collect; we suggest reporting them alongside task reward.

</details>


### [69] [The Kinetics of Training: A Driven-Nucleation Rate Law for Emergence, Plasticity Loss, and Circuit Control in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27281)
*Lei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示语言模型能力涌现的速率限制步骤是"无部分信用的联合对齐"——即电路最后部分必须在单次随机尝试中对齐，而"只差一个正确"毫无价值。作者通过五部分电路与三部分电路缺失三部分时等待时间相近（1.19-1.37）、以及在Pythia模型上裁剪单个部件后仅保留17%能力（部分信用预测为50-83%，p=2e-10）等指纹证明这一机理，并提出一个以缺失部件数为指数障碍项的速率方程，通过前向（预测催化和点火）、后向（学习延迟与不可点火）以及机制修复（重初始化QK切片可恢复可学习性）三路验证，最终在门控注意力模型上证明该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型中能力涌现的根本机制，特别是为什么能力会"突然"出现而非渐进增长。作者发现现有理解存在盲区：当电路多部件协同工作时，仅靠部分匹配的努力（部分信用）并不足以形成能力，这种"全对或全错"的联合对齐过程才是能力形成的瓶颈，但这一过程此前未被独立刻画和量化。

Method: 方法包括：（1）在无短路设定的装置上测量等待时间与缺失部件数的关系，区分"缺失数决定瓶颈"而非"电路规模决定瓶颈"；（2）在Pythia模型上跨7种能力和3种规模做消融实验，对比真实能力保留度与部分信用预测值；（3）建立速率方程：sites × attempts × drive × exp(-beta*K) 减去 destruction，以冻结常数做三路预注册验证——前向点火/浓度预报、后向延迟学习、机制定位与修复；（4）在受控门控注意力模型中证明机制；（5）用满足涨落-耗散关系的噪声替换SGD噪声，实现电路的退火、熔融和定时钉扎。

Result: 实验结果显示：在无短路装置中五部分电路缺失三部分的等待时间与三部分电路缺失三部分相近（比率1.19-1.37），证明瓶颈由缺失部件数而非电路规模决定；在Pythia的32/32个判别性单元中，消融单一部分后能力中位数仅保留17%，远低于部分信用预测的50-83%（p=2e-10），而随机非部分头保留100%。速率方程三路预注册验证全部通过：前向预测在10/10模型上点火、0/12模型低于浓度下限，仍平坦时可通过前驱体以5%中位误差预判到达时间；后向显示延迟学习随等待增长，超过临界步后永不点火，且验证损失始终平滑下降使标准监控失效；重初始化QK切片恢复可学习性（6/6）而V切片无效（0/6）；门控注意力模型证明occupation造成deadline的机制无需混合假设。

Conclusion: 能力涌现的关键并非电路部件的逐步改善，而是所有关键部分在单次尝试中同时就位的"无部分信用联合对齐"事件，其速率由缺失部件数K以指数形式控制（exp(-beta*K)）。该机制解释了为什么标准单调监控（如验证损失）对能力涌现的临界点不可见，并为干预提供了精确位点（QK切片重初始化）和定时策略（满足涨落-耗散关系的退火式噪声调度）。论文确立了一个从宏观速率方程到微观电路机制、再到可控实验验证的完整证据链，将该机理普适性地推广至1.4B规模的变换器。

Abstract: A capability appears in a language model when the last parts of its circuit align in one stochastic attempt, and getting all but one right is worth nothing. We show this no-partial-credit joint alignment is the rate-limiting step of capability formation. Two fingerprints: in a shortcut-free apparatus a five-part circuit missing three waits as long as a three-part circuit missing three (1.19-1.37), so the wait counts missing parts, not size; and on Pythia across seven capabilities and three scales, ablating one part leaves a median 17% of the capability in 32 of 32 discriminating cells, where partial credit predicts 50-83% (p = 2e-10), while a random non-part head leaves 100%.
  One rare event whose barrier grows with missing parts yields a rate equation -- sites x attempts x drive x exp(-beta*K), minus destruction -- read three ways, each preregistered with frozen constants. Forward: a capability flat at baseline ignites at a step of our choosing once the mix passes a concentration floor (10/10 above, 0/12 below), and while still flat its arrival is datable from its precursor to 5% median error on six held-out models. Backward: the delay to learn a withheld capability grows with waiting until, past a critical step, it never ignites -- yet validation loss falls smoothly throughout, so standard monitors are blind to it. We locate the damage (heads commit to the base data) and isolate the cure: re-initializing only the query-key slices restores learnability (6/6) while the value slices do nothing (0/6). We prove the mechanism in a controlled gated-attention model: occupation forces a deadline whose consequences need no mixing assumption. Completed: SGD's noise fails the fluctuation-dissipation test, so we install one and anneal, melt and pin circuits on schedule. Scope: conjunction circuits in transformers to 1.4B.

</details>


### [70] [Benchmarking the Residual: What Long-Horizon Evaluations Add Beyond Matched Short-Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27283)
*Chao Peng,Zhiheng Lyu,Peijie Dong,Hande Dong,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"轨迹诱导退化"概念，指早期执行使后续工作更困难的现象（当有害累积特指模型可见文本时称为"context rot"）。作者主张：若要声称存在"长时程失败"，基准测试必须将实际完整任务成功率与基于短、单个阶段构建的基线预测进行比较，其对数比值称为"horizon residual"。该比较需使用相同智能体配置，并预先指定阶段、检查点、信息和预算的选择方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程基准测试常显示任务越长智能体越容易失败，但这一观察本身无法解释失败原因。失败可能源于三个阶段性问题：更多阶段导致普通错误累积、更长任务包含更难的单个决策、或随着对话历史/工具输出/环境变化累积而变得更难（即轨迹诱导退化）。作者旨在为"长时程失败"提供严谨的归因方法。

Method: 本文是立场论文（position paper），提出方法论框架：1）定义轨迹诱导退化（trajectory-induced degradation），特指前期执行使后续工作变难的情况，若有害累积是模型可见文本则称为context rot；2）提出"horizon residual"指标，即实际完整任务成功率与由短、独立阶段构建的基线预测之间的对数比值；3）要求比较时使用相同智能体配置，并预先明确阶段划分、检查点、信息流和预算的设定方式。

Result: horizon residual能够量化完整rollout与所选基线之间的差异，从而判断是否存在超越普通错误累积的长时程失效。但残差本身只能表明差异存在，仍需针对性的对照实验来确定具体解释机制（如context rot、阶段间耦合或单阶段难度变化）。

Conclusion: 作者主张长时程失败不能仅凭"任务越长失败越多"来认定，必须通过horizon residual这样的严格量化比较来验证。该框架为长时程智能体评估建立了更严谨的方法论标准，但归因解释仍需依赖针对性实验。

Abstract: Long-horizon benchmarks often show that agents fail more as tasks become longer. This observation is useful for deployment, but it does not by itself explain why failure occurs. More stages create more opportunities for ordinary errors to compound; longer tasks may also contain harder individual decisions or become harder as conversation history, tool outputs, and environment changes accumulate. We use trajectory-induced degradation to mean this last possibility: earlier execution makes later work harder. When the harmful accumulation is specifically the text visible to the model, it is often called context rot. In this position paper, we argue that to claim a "long-horizon failure", benchmarks must compare actual full-task success against a baseline prediction built from short, individual stages. We call the log-ratio between this prediction and actual success the horizon residual. The comparison must use the same agent configuration and specify in advance how stages, checkpoints, information, and budgets will be chosen. The residual shows that the full rollout differs from the chosen baseline; targeted experiments are still needed to explain why.

</details>


### [71] [TIER-MoE: Trust-Informed Expert Routing via Conditional Modality Risk for Multimodal Fusion in Biomedical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27289)
*Yu Chang,Anzhe Cheng,Chenwei Wu,Zhuoran Wang,Jiahao Chen,Tamoghna Chattopadhyay,Sophia I. Thomopoulos,Paul M. Thompson,Liyue Shen,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIER-MoE是一种风险引导的子空间混合专家模型，通过将模态可靠性定义为单模态预测器的预期损失，结合专家子空间兼容性进行稀疏路由，在四个公开生物医学多模态数据集上取得了优于现有方法的预测性能和概率校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合承诺结合互补证据，但更多证据并不总是带来更好的预测。现有的多模态模型虽然通过更丰富的跨模态交互和样本自适应融合取得了进展，但模态在融合中的权重并不能揭示该来源是否不可靠、冗余或与专业专家不匹配。

Method: TIER-MoE将模态可靠性定义为单模态预测器预期损失的风险，该风险通过未在对应样本上训练的模型生成的out-of-fold预测来学习。模型将估计的风险与专家特定的子空间兼容性结合用于稀疏模态-专家路由，同时一个始终激活的共享路径保持多模态互补性。

Result: 在四个公开多模态生物医学数据集（涵盖阿尔茨海默病状态、皮肤病变恶性程度和视网膜分类）上，TIER-MoE在预测性能和概率校准方面优于现有最先进方法，在Macro-F1和Brier分数上有一致改进，并在外部队列上展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: TIER-MoE通过风险引导的稀疏路由机制有效区分了模态的不可靠、冗余与不匹配问题，不仅提升了多模态融合的预测性能，还改善了概率校准，并具有良好的外部泛化能力。

Abstract: The promise of multimodal fusion lies in combining complementary sources of evidence, yet more evidence does not always yield a better prediction. Recent multimodal models have advanced fusion through richer cross-modal interaction and sample-adaptive fusion. However, the influence assigned to a modality during fusion does not reveal whether that source is unreliable, redundant, or poorly matched to a specialized expert. To address this limitation, we introduce TIER-MoE, a risk-guided subspace mixture-of-experts model that defines sample-specific modality reliability as the prediction loss its unimodal predictor is expected to incur. This risk is learned from out-of-fold predictions generated by models that were not trained on the corresponding sample. TIER-MoE combines the estimated risk with expert-specific subspace compatibility for sparse modality-expert routing, while an always-active shared path preserves multimodal complementarity. We evaluate TIER-MoE on four public multimodal biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease status, skin-lesion malignancy, and retinal classification. Results demonstrate its superiority over state-of-the-art methods in predictive performance and probability calibration, with consistent improvements in Macro-F1 and Brier score and strong zero-shot generalization to an external cohort.

</details>


### [72] [Strategies for Milestone-driven Start-ups in Multi-activity Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27563)
*Zhengli Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究里程碑导向下初创企业的最优策略。通过扩散过程刻画企业状态，企业家可在多种成本与风险各异的控制（活动）间选择，企业达到上边界即成功、下边界即失败。作者完整求解了多控制（≥3）连续时间随机控制问题，提出"效率前沿曲线"（按风险率drift-to-volatility和成本效率drift-to-cost排序控制），并首次发现该曲线可因参数不同出现不同类型，导致最优策略结构发生质变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新创企业需要通过多个里程碑目标才能生存，但现有研究缺乏对里程碑导向场景下多控制随机控制问题的完整求解，尤其是≥3个控制的情形在数学上极为困难，亟需可解析刻画的最优策略理论。

Method: 构建扩散过程状态模型，企业在多种控制（不同成本和drift/方差影响）间选择；引入效率前沿曲线对控制排序，通过两个直观度量（drift-to-volatility比的风险性和drift-to-cost比的成本效益）刻画最优控制集；系统分析模型参数如何影响效率前沿曲线的类型及对应的最优策略结构。

Result: 完整求解了多控制随机控制问题，给出最优策略的显式结构刻画。最优策略仅使用效率前沿曲线所确定的控制集合。更重要的是，根据模型参数不同，效率前沿曲线可分为不同类型，导致最优策略结构在质上相异——这是首个允许效率前沿曲线存在不同类型的随机控制模型。

Conclusion: 为初创企业提供了评估活动的直观度量，揭示了里程碑导向下最优策略如何随具体情境发生质变，为创业决策研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: New venture start-ups need to ``survive'' through multiple stages of reaching milestone targets. We investigate the strategies for start-ups in a milestone-oriented setting. We examine a model of an entrepreneurial start-up firm, where its state is captured by a diffusion process. The entrepreneur can choose between multiple activities (or controls), which incur different cost and determine the drift and the variance of the process. Depending on whether the process reaches a fixed upper boundary or a lower one, the start-up firm succeeds or fails. Continuous-time stochastic models with multiple ($\ge 3$) controls are typically very challenging to deal with. In this work, we are able to completely solve for the optimal policy and provide an explicit characterization of its structure. In particular, the optimal policy only uses controls from a set characterized by a so-called efficient frontier curve that orders the controls by two intuitive measures: riskiness (drift-to-volatility ratio) and cost-effectiveness (drift-to-cost ratio). A unique feature of our model is that depending on the model parameters, the efficient frontier curves can be of different types, resulting in qualitatively different structures of the optimal policy. As far as we know, this is the first study that analyzes a stochastic control model which admits efficient frontier curves of different types. Our work provides start-up firms with intuitive measures to evaluate their activities and offers valuable insights on how the optimal strategies in a milestone-oriented setting change qualitatively contingent upon the specific scenario. We believe the results provide a foundational block in the study of entrepreneurial decision-making.

</details>


### [73] [THGFM: Dual-Branch Temporal Heterogeneous Graph Fusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27303)
*Yixin Peng,Diego Collarana,Er Jin,Stefan Decker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: THGFM是一个面向web规模时序异构图的双路径融合模型，通过共享空间时序注意力与关系类型分区时序注意力的耦合，结合双路径融合机制和旋转时序注意力，在学术图基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序异构图表征学习方法存在两个局限：一是难以在参数高效的跨类型迁移与关系感知特化之间取得平衡；二是时间信息通常仅作为加性特征注入，未直接参与注意力核的计算。

Method: THGFM采用统一双路径架构：(1) Shared-Space Temporal Attention分支实现参数高效的跨类型迁移；(2) Relational Type-Partitioned Temporal Attention分支实现关系感知特化；(3) 通过Type-Conditioned Non-Competitive Gated Sum Fusion进行双路径融合，为共享和特化分支分配独立的、类型条件的特征门控，避免零和竞争；(4) 提出Rotary Temporal Attention，在注意力匹配前利用相对时间对查询和键进行半相位旋转，直接将相对时间嵌入注意力分数计算。

Result: THGFM在学术图基准上持续优于基线图Transformer模型：六任务平均提升+3.25%，峰值相对增益在OAG-CS PV上达+12.37%，PF-L2为+4.87%，PF-L1为+1.18%，OGBN-MAG、HTAG-ArXiv和HTAG-DBLP上分别提升+4.24%、+3.73%和+4.61%。

Conclusion: THGFM通过双路径架构同时解决了跨类型参数共享与关系特化的矛盾，并通过旋转时序注意力将时间信息直接融入注意力计算中，验证了该框架在异构时序图学习上的有效性。

Abstract: Temporal heterogeneous graphs offer a natural abstraction for dynamic relational systems in which diverse node and relation types co-exist and evolve over time. Learning on such graphs requires jointly modeling cross-type structural heterogeneity and the temporal dynamics of interactions, yet existing methods still struggle to reconcile parameter-efficient cross-type transfer with relation-aware specialization, and typically inject time only as additive features outside the attention kernel. We propose \textbf{THGFM}, a web-scale temporal heterogeneous graph fusion model that addresses both limitations within a unified dual-path architecture. THGFM couples a \textit{Shared-Space Temporal Attention} branch for parameter-efficient cross-type transfer with a \textit{Relational Type-Partitioned Temporal Attention} branch for relation-aware specialization, and integrates them through \textit{Dual-Path Relational--Shared Fusion}, instantiated with \textit{Type-Conditioned Non-Competitive Gated Sum Fusion}: a adaptive mechanism that assigns independent, type-conditioned feature-wise gates to the shared and specialized branches, allowing both to be amplified or suppressed without zero-sum competition. To directly incorporate relative time into the attention score, THGFM further introduces \textit{Rotary Temporal Attention}, which rotates queries and keys by half-phases of relative time before matching. THGFM consistently outperforms baseline graph transformer models on academic graphs benchmarks, delivering a $+3.25\%$ six-task mean gain, with peak relative gains of $+12.37\%$ on OAG-CS PV, $+4.87\%$ on PF-$L_2$, and $+1.18\%$ on PF-$L_1$, and $+4.24\%$, $+3.73\%$, and $+4.61\%$ on OGBN-MAG, HTAG-ArXiv, and HTAG-DBLP, respectively.

</details>


### [74] [Equilibrium Training of Energy-Based Models with Parallel Trajectory Tempering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27077)
*Nicolas Béreux,Aurélien Decelle,Cyril Furtlehner,Beatriz Seoane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于平行轨迹回火（PTT）的训练算法，通过利用优化路径的连续性维持平衡采样，使能量模型（EBM）在多模态、数据稀缺的科学数据集上实现稳定快速的训练，计算成本与持久对比散度相当，并能在受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）和离散表格数据上超越现有EBM训练方法及深度生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量模型（EBMs）为科学数据生成提供了可解释的框架，但马可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）混合不良往往限制了其可靠性，导致训练不稳定且在实际应用中受限。

Method: 提出平行轨迹回火（PTT）训练算法：利用优化路径的连续性在训练全程维持平衡采样；结合储层采样（reservoir sampling）和自适应优化，计算成本与持久对比散度（PCD）相当；可提供热化时间（thermalization times）的直接估计、训练模型的平衡样本及准确的对数似然。

Result: 在受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）上，PTT一致性地优于现有EBM训练方法；在离散表格数据上，超越最先进的深度生成模型，生成样本质量更高，对过拟合和数据有限的鲁棒性更强。

Conclusion: PTT使EBM的平衡最大似然训练变得实用且计算高效，为科学数据生成提供可靠且可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: Energy-Based Models (EBMs) provide an interpretable framework for generative modeling of scientific data, but poor Markov Chain Monte Carlo mixing often limits their reliability. We introduce a training algorithm based on Parallel Trajectory Tempering (PTT), which exploits the continuity of the optimization path to maintain equilibrium sampling throughout learning. This enables stable and fast training on highly multimodal and data-scarce scientific datasets. Combined with reservoir sampling and adaptive optimization, PTT has a computational cost comparable to Persistent Contrastive Divergence, making it a practical replacement for standard training methods. It also provides direct estimates of thermalization times, equilibrium samples from trained models, and accurate log-likelihoods at essentially no additional cost. Experiments on Restricted Boltzmann Machines show that PTT consistently outperforms existing EBM training approaches. On discrete tabular data, it also surpasses state-of-the-art deep generative models, yielding higher-quality samples and greater robustness to overfitting and limited data. Our results make equilibrium maximum-likelihood training of EBMs practical and computationally efficient.

</details>


### [75] [EvoCause: LLM-Guided Evolution of Causal Graphs for Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27290)
*Lei Zan,Keli Zhang,Shifeng Xie,Jiale Zheng,Zehao Xiao,Zhiwei Dong,Ke Zhang,Ruichu Cai,Malik Tiomoko,Lujia Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoCause，一种利用专家诊断标签和大语言模型（LLM）迭代优化因果图进行根因分析（RCA）的方法。EvoCause通过LLM提出语义上合理的图编辑方案，用确定性代码验证节点身份和无环性，并在标注数据集上选出最优图。同时发布了TeleRCA基准数据集，包含来自生产电信网络的485,681条告警事件。实验表明EvoCause在合成数据和真实数据上都优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于因果图的根因分析方法在从观测日志中学习因果图后，图是固定不变的，无法利用历史事件中专家诊断的知识来改进图的质量。这导致图可能偏离真实因果结构，影响根因定位的准确性。需要一种方法将专家知识闭环地引入因果图学习和优化过程。

Method: EvoCause采用人机协作的迭代优化框架：1) 专家对历史事件提供根因标签，约束哪些告警应为源节点；2) 使用LLM提出语义上合理的图编辑（增删边等操作）；3) 用确定性代码验证编辑后的图是否满足节点身份一致性和无环性约束；4) 在带标签的对齐集上评估并保留最佳图作为下一代基础。测试时仅使用优化后的图进行推理，无需LLM调用，保证透明性和效率。

Result: 在合成数据上，使用PC算法初始化的EvoCause相比未优化的PC基线，Node F1提升11.59个百分点，Case EM提升9.40个百分点，Graph F1提升4.59个百分点，nSHD降低0.2379。在TeleRCA真实数据集上，将可读的告警标题替换为匿名标识符后，Node F1和Case EM分别下降6.12和8.04个百分点，表明告警名称的语义信息对图细化有显著贡献。

Conclusion: EvoCause成功地将专家知识和LLM的语义理解能力引入因果图优化闭环，显著提升了根因分析的准确性。实验同时表明告警名称的可读语义信息在图编辑过程中发挥重要作用，为未来的RCA系统设计提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: Modern telecommunication, cloud, and microservice systems emit correlated alarm cascades when components fail. Root cause analysis (RCA) aims to identify the small set of alarms that initiate each cascade. A common approach learns a causal graph from observational logs and predicts all zero-in-degree alarms in each incident-induced subgraph. However, the learned graph remains fixed and cannot benefit from expert diagnoses of historical incidents. We close this loop with EvoCause. Expert labels constrain which alarms should be source nodes but do not specify the edge edits needed to satisfy those constraints. EvoCause uses a large language model (LLM) to propose semantically plausible graph edits, while deterministic code validates node identities and acyclicity and retains the best graph on a labeled alignment set. At test time, the refined graph alone produces transparent predictions without an LLM call. We also release TeleRCA, an expert-annotated benchmark from a production telecommunication network containing $485{,}681$ alarm events spanning $194$ alarm types over $5{,}621$ resources. On synthetic data, EvoCause initialized with the PC causal discovery algorithm outperforms the unrefined PC baseline, raising Node F1, Case EM, and Graph F1 by $11.59$, $9.40$, and $4.59$ percentage points, respectively, while reducing nSHD by $0.2379$. On TeleRCA, replacing human-readable alarm titles with anonymous identifiers lowers Node F1 and Case EM by $6.12$ and $8.04$ percentage points, respectively, indicating that alarm-name information contributes to graph refinement.

</details>


### [76] [Towards joint scaling laws with optimal batch size schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27731)
*Jiaxiang Li,Zhiqi Bu,Shiyun Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过凸优化的视角研究了深度学习训练中学习率和批量大小对训练动态的联合影响，推导出损失函数的联合表征，并据此给出任意学习率下最优批量大小调度的闭式解，提出联合缩放定律，在LLM训练中优于静态批量大小的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习通常在整个训练过程中保持批量大小不变，忽视了学习率和批量大小对训练动态的联合影响，缺乏对两者协同作用的系统性理解。

Method: 通过凸优化的视角研究深度学习训练动态，推导损失函数关于学习率和批量大小调度的联合表征，适用于通用优化器和模型架构；基于该表征推导出任意学习率调度下最优批量大小调度的闭式解。

Result: 提出的联合缩放定律在不同配置下一致优于静态批量大小的基线方法，突出了动态批量大小调度在大语言模型训练中的重要性。

Conclusion: 动态调整批量大小调度能够显著提升大规模模型训练的效率，静态批量大小训练方式存在优化空间，学习率与批量大小的联合调度是提升训练性能的关键。

Abstract: Modern deep learning typically keeps the batch size static throughout training, thus overlooking the joint effect of learning rate and batch size on the training dynamics. In this paper, we study the deep learning dynamics through the lens of convex optimization and derive a joint characterization of loss in terms of both schedules, applicable to general optimizers and model architectures. This characterization yields a closed-form optimal batch size schedule for any prescribed learning rate schedule, and further leads to joint scaling laws that consistently outperform static batch size baselines, highlighting the significance of dynamic batch size schedule in large language model training.

</details>


### [77] [Generalization Bounds on Optimal Control for Transformer Training and Wasserstein Distributional Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27975)
*Kağan Akman,Naci Saldi,Serdar Yüksel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为使用动态规划递归训练的Transformer推导了有限样本泛化界。通过将数据集视为经验输入-输出测度对的概率定律，将训练问题解释为有限时域马尔可夫控制问题，并利用量化模型、集中不等式和Lipschitz稳定性估计导出显式泛化界，最后将结果联系到Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在广泛任务中表现出色，但其理论保证，尤其是使用动态规划递归训练时的有限样本泛化界尚缺乏严格分析。现有工作大多关注标准自监督或监督训练，而对动态规划形式的训练缺乏理论刻画。作者希望通过动态规划视角建立Transformer泛化性的理论框架。

Method: 1) 采用双重提升、测度值形式的Transformer动态表述，将数据集建模为经验输入-输出测度对上的概率定律；2) 将训练问题转化为有限时域马尔可夫控制问题；3) 对状态、动作和测度状态空间进行量化，得到量化模型；4) 利用有限度量空间上经验定律的集中不等式结合值函数的Lipschitz稳定性估计，推导显式有限样本泛化界；5) 通过显式逼近误差将量化模型的界转移到基模型；6) 利用相同机制将训练问题表述为Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化问题。

Result: 推导出Transformer在动态规划递归训练下的显式有限样本泛化界，并量化了从量化模型转移到原始模型的逼近误差。此外，建立了Transformer泛化与Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化之间的理论联系。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为动态规划训练的Transformer提供了严格的有限样本泛化保证，并通过分布鲁棒优化的视角深化了对Transformer泛化机制的理解，为后续理论研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We derive finite-sample generalization bounds for Transformers trained with dynamic programming recursions. Building on the doubly lifted, measure-valued formulation of Transformer dynamics, we view data sets as probability laws on pairs of empirical input-output measures, allowing us to interpret the training problem as a finite-horizon Markovian control problem. We then analyze a quantized model, derived by quantizing the state, action, and measure-state spaces, and derive explicit finite-sample generalization bounds using concentration inequalities for empirical laws on finite metric spaces together with a Lipschitz stability estimate for the value function. These bounds are transferred to the base model at the cost of an explicit approximation error.
