Motivation: 神经网络越来越多地合并跨群体、跨时期和跨工况的数据以提升泛化能力，但池化重拟合是否保留各数据源各自支持的偏好排序成为一个关键的可靠性问题。CBDT的组合公理形式化了这一需求——来源支持的偏好应在其并集下得以保留。研究旨在系统性地探究该属性在固定表征+OLS输出头的神经网络中何时成立。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明池化重拟合本质上是重算用于加权源证据的逆Gram几何，推导出偏好保留的精确与近似条件；2) 提出尺度不变的Gram失配度量用于候选池优先级排序，并提出面向几何的正则化用于在训练过程中塑造源几何；3) 开发三阶段审计框架，通过决策变化追溯到任务定义的效用损失，追踪严格的成对反转。

Result: 在负载竞价代理及医学、金融决策代理上的实验揭示了稳定与易反转的池化机制：负载审计在代理效用下识别出可测量的非零类"源共识相对有害决策"；跨域审计表明相似的失配可对应截然不同的保留率；面向几何的目标在描述准确率、一致性、几何和危害之间占据不同的运行点。

Conclusion: 该框架通过筛选、解析认证、面向几何的训练以及决策后果审计，使组合可靠性变得可测量且可操作，为跨源池化场景下的神经网络决策可靠性提供了系统化的理论支撑和工程方法。

Abstract: Neural networks increasingly combine data across populations, time periods, and operating conditions to improve generalization. This raises a reliability question: whether a model refitted on pooled data preserves an action ordering supported by both sources. Case-Based Decision Theory (CBDT) formalizes this requirement through its composition axiom, which requires source-supported preferences to survive their union. We study when this property holds for fixed-representation neural networks with ordinary least squares (OLS) output heads. First, we show that pooled refitting recomputes the inverse-Gram geometry used to weight source evidence, which can reverse shared preferences, and derive exact and approximate preservation conditions. Next, we introduce a scale-invariant Gram mismatch measure for prioritizing candidate pools and geometry-oriented regularization for shaping source geometry during training. Finally, we develop a three-stage audit that traces strict pairwise reversals through decision changes to task-defined utility loss. Experiments spanning a load-based bidding proxy and medical and financial decision proxies reveal stable and reversal-prone pooling regimes: the load audit identifies a measurable nonzero class of source-consensus-relative harmful decisions under the proxy utility, while cross-domain audits show that comparable mismatch can correspond to sharply different preservation rates. Geometry-oriented objectives occupy distinct descriptive accuracy-consistency-geometry-harm operating points. Together, the framework makes compositional reliability measurable and operational through screening, analytic certification, geometry-oriented training, and decision-consequence auditing.

</details>


### [176] [Expected Survival-Time Bounds for Robust Optimization Over Time under Isotropic Gaussian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27280)
*Pavel Novoa-Hernández*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Robust Optimization Over Time (ROOT) is a recent branch of evolutionary dynamic optimization that seeks solutions capable of remaining effective across multiple consecutive environments. Unlike the traditional track-the-moving-optimum (TMO) paradigm, which reoptimizes after every environmental change, ROOT explicitly values persistence. Although the field has grown considerably, most contributions remain algorithmic and empirical, leaving several fundamental properties poorly understood from a theoretical perspective. One such property is survival time, defined as the number of future environments in which a deployed solution continues to satisfy a prescribed quality threshold. While survival time is widely used as a measure of temporal robustness, little is known about how its expected value depends on environmental dynamics, deployment quality, or problem characteristics. This paper studies expected survival time for a fixed deployed solution under isotropic Gaussian environmental dynamics. Modeling survival as a discrete first-exit problem, we derive a rigorous lower bound and a computable multi-step upper bound. The analysis shows that expected survival scales as $Θ(σ^-{2})$ in slowly varying environments and approaches its minimum value of one future change in high dimensions. A comprehensive Monte Carlo study validates the theoretical predictions, examines sensitivity to modeling assumptions and parameter uncertainty, and illustrates how the bounds can support deployment decisions after optimization. The resulting framework provides an analytical characterization of deployment lifetime and identifies when a required deployment horizon can be guaranteed, ruled out, or remains analytically unresolved.

</details>


### [177] [An analysis of binary isotonic regression: degrees of freedom and implications for calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27301)
*Raphael Rossellini,Rina Foygel Barber,Zhimei Ren,Jake A. Soloff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对二元样本上保序回归的任意最坏情况自由度给出了完全精确的有限样本刻画，识别出使保序回归拟合值数量最大化的二元序列，并通过解析数论得到了以3/(4π²)^(1/3)·n^(2/3)为首项的尖锐界。进一步将该结果应用于校准，推导出保序回归期望校准误差(ECE)的首个非平凡、完全无分布假设的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保序回归是估计单调函数和校准概率预测器的经典工具，但其在有限样本下的最坏情况自由度尚未被精确刻画。此外，校准是概率预测的核心要求，而之前缺乏关于保序回归期望校准误差的分布无关的理论保证。

Method: 利用解析数论的技术推导保序回归在二元样本上的最坏情况自由度的尖锐有限样本界；基于确定性自由度界，进一步推导保序回归的期望校准误差(ECE)的无分布保证。

Result: 得到了保序回归自由度的完全尖锐的有限样本刻画，前导项为3/(4π²)^(1/3)·n^(2/3)，优于此前已知的界；同时导出了首个非平凡的、模型无关且分布无关的ECE保证，仅需假设Y∈{0,1}。

Conclusion: 本文通过精确刻画保序回归在最坏情况下的自由度，显著改进了理论界，并首次为保序回归的期望校准误差提供了完全分布自由的保证，对概率预测校准的理论基础做出了重要推进。

Abstract: Isotonic regression is a canonical tool for estimating monotone functions and calibrating probabilistic predictors. We provide a fully sharp finite-sample characterization of its worst-case degrees of freedom on binary samples. Specifically, we identify the binary sequences that maximize the number of distinct fitted values produced by isotonic regression. We develop a sharp bound on the degrees of freedom with a leading term of $\frac{3}{(4π^2)^{1/3}} n^{2/3}$ using analytic number theory, improving on previous bounds.
  We then apply this result to calibration. Calibration is a central requirement for probabilistic prediction, and isotonic regression is a widely used post-processing method for improving calibration. Building on deterministic degrees-of-freedom bounds, we derive, to our knowledge, the first nontrivial distribution-free guarantee on the Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of isotonic regression. This ECE bound is fully model-free and distribution-free, only assuming $Y \in \{0,1\}$.

</details>


### [178] [HOMER: Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27532)
*Kisung You,Boram Cho*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出HOMER（Huber-of-Means）方法，通过径向Huber中心聚合块均值，在截断程度为0时退化为样本均值，随着阈值增大从稳健的中位数式估计平滑过渡到均值式估计，并建立了Hilbert空间多数定理和MOM阶偏差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾分布会削弱经验均值的高置信控制能力，而几何中位数-of-means（MOM）方法缺乏一个能向均值效率靠拢的阈值参数，导致无法在稳健性和效率之间灵活调节。

Method: HOMER将数据分块后计算块均值，再通过径向Huber中心聚合这些块均值。文中给出canonical和pseudo两种Huber形式，均能限制每个块得分并实现中位数式稳健性到经验均值之间的插值；同时推导了Hilbert空间多数定理和MOM阶偏差界，并在有限三阶矩条件下允许固定维投影以参数速率进行均值推断，要求块大小和块数同时增长且块大小增长更快。

Result: Canonical HOMER在其二次区域内恢复样本均值，Pseudo-HOMER随阈值增大趋向样本均值，并具备渐近线性和一致的三明治协方差估计。重尾模拟显示当少数块摘要被置换时HOMER保持稳定；在干净高斯数据上两种版本均接近经验均值效率；有限块三明治区间存在欠覆盖问题（尤其对偏斜函数型数据）；当污染影响大多数块或破坏普通块内均值时方法失效。

Conclusion: HOMER提供了一种在稳健性和均值效率之间可调谐的估计方案，在多数块完好时具有良好表现，但需注意其有限样本区间覆盖的不足以及污染范围过大时方法的局限性。

Abstract: Heavy tails weaken high-confidence control for the empirical mean. Geometric median-of-means (MOM) also lacks a threshold that moves toward mean efficiency. We propose \emph{HOMER}, or Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation. HOMER aggregates block means through a radial Huber center. Its canonical and pseudo-Huber forms bound each block score and interpolate between median-like robustness and the empirical mean. We establish a Hilbert-space majority theorem and a MOM-order deviation bound under a finite second moment. Canonical HOMER recovers the sample mean inside its quadratic region. Pseudo-HOMER approaches the sample mean as the threshold grows. It also admits asymptotic linearity and consistent sandwich covariance estimation around the population block-Huber target. Under a finite third moment, fixed finite-dimensional projections support mean inference at the usual parametric rate. This result requires growing block sizes and counts, with block sizes increasing faster. Heavy-tailed simulations show that HOMER remains stable when a minority of block summaries is displaced. On clean Gaussian data, both versions closely approach the empirical mean's efficiency. Finite-block sandwich intervals undercovered, especially for skewed functional data. Further studies show failure when contamination affects most blocks or compromises ordinary within-block means.

</details>


### [179] [Robust Wavelength Selection for Partial Least Squares Sugar Content Estimation Using Combinatorial Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27645)
*Mitsunobu Kanebako,Ami S. Koshikawa,Masaru Hitomi,Takuro Tanaka,Mahito Chiba,Maiko Mori,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于贝叶斯优化的波长区域选择方法（稀疏二次代理模型+Thompson采样），将近红外光谱糖分含量估计的波长选择问题建模为二元黑盒优化问题，通过模拟/量子退火求解QUBO问题，实验表明该方法优于遗传算法和模拟退火，具有更好的预测精度和局部稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外光谱分析中，波长选择是提升预测精度和可解释性的关键预处理步骤。传统方法（如遗传算法、模拟退火）容易过拟合且结果不稳定，需要一种更鲁棒的波长区域选择方法。

Method: 将波长区域选择建模为二元黑盒组合优化问题；构建稀疏二次代理模型；通过Thompson采样顺序提取感兴趣波长区域；最小化采集函数作为QUBO问题，由模拟退火或量子退火求解。

Result: 实验表明该方法在偏最小二乘回归中提升了预测精度，比遗传算法和模拟退火产生更一致的波长区域；在一位局部扰动下均方根误差波动极小，表明收敛到更平滑的误差曲面，避免了孤立过拟合解。

Conclusion: 组合贝叶斯优化是光谱预测任务中稳健特征选择的有效框架，能够在提升预测精度的同时保证选择结果的稳定性和可解释性。

Abstract: Wavelength selection is one of the important preprocessing methods in near-infrared spectroscopy to improve prediction accuracy and interpretability of spectral data. We formulate wavelength-region selection for sugar content estimation as a binary black-box optimization problem and propose a method based on Bayesian optimization. The proposed method constructs a sparse quadratic surrogate model and sequentially extracts interested wavelength regions by Thompson sampling. Minimizing an acquisition function is performed as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization problem by simulated or quantum annealing. Experiments show that the proposed method improves the prediction accuracy of partial least squares regression and yields more consistent wavelength regions than genetic-algorithm-based selection and simulated annealing. Under one-bit local perturbations, the selected wavelength regions show minimal fluctuations in root mean square errors between observed and predicted values of a validation set. This local stability suggests that our method converges to a smoother error landscape and avoids isolated overfitted solutions. These results indicate that combinatorial Bayesian optimization is a useful framework for robust feature selection in spectroscopic prediction tasks.

</details>


### [180] [Error Analysis of Neural-Network-Based Engression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27723)
*Juntong Chen,Zijian Guo,Xinwei Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对深度神经网络实现的engression方法（基于能量分数的条件分布生成模型）进行了理论误差分析，将超额风险分解为近似误差、随机误差和蒙特卡洛误差三部分，并在目标条件生成器满足组合光滑性结构的假设下建立了收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Engression是一种通过学习生成模型Y=f(X,ε)在能量分数下拟合条件分布的新方法，但此前缺乏其理论误差分析。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为engression方法提供严格的误差界和收敛率保证。

Method: 将engression的超额风险分解为三个独立组成部分：近似误差（approximation error，衡量网络表达能力）、随机误差（stochastic error，衡量有限样本估计偏差）和蒙特卡洛误差（Monte Carlo error，衡量能量分数的蒙特卡洛估计偏差），并基于目标条件生成器的组合光滑性结构假设推导各部分的收敛速率。

Result: 在组合光滑性假设下，建立了engression深度神经网络的收敛速率，给出了超额风险三个组成项各自的误差界及整体收敛率。

Conclusion: 通过系统的误差分解，为engression方法提供了坚实的理论基础，证明了其在组合光滑性条件下的一致性收敛，为该方法的实际应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Engression (Shen and Meinshausen, 2024) learns a conditional distribution by fitting a generative model $Y = f(X,\varepsilon)$ under the energy score, a strictly proper scoring rule. We provide a theoretical error analysis of engression implemented with deep neural networks. We decompose the excess risk into three components: the approximation error, the stochastic error, and the Monte Carlo error. Based on this decomposition, we establish convergence rates under the assumption that the target conditional generator admits a compositional smoothness structure.

</details>


### [181] [Robust Estimation of Sparse Numerical Vectors under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27815)
*Puning Zhao,Zhikun Zhang,Shaowei Wang,Sheng Yue,Bangzhou Xin,Tianhang Zheng,Pengfei Zhang,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多物品用户本地差分隐私（LDP）协议中毒攻击的鲁棒稀疏向量均值估计方法——随机投影与裁剪（RPC）。该方法通过服务器向用户发送随机二进制向量、用户投影并裁剪本地数据来限制攻击者能力，并通过精确分析偏差表达式来消除偏差-方差权衡的依赖，从而进一步缩小裁剪阈值以增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LDP防御策略主要针对单物品用户，但在实际中用户可能拥有多个物品（即稀疏向量）。由于多物品场景下输出空间更大，攻击者可以发动更强大的中毒攻击而不易被发现，这使得防御变得更加困难。

Method: 提出Randomized Projection with Clipping (RPC)方法。具体步骤包括：(1) 服务器向每个用户发送随机二进制向量；(2) 用户将本地数据投影到该向量上并裁剪数值以限制攻击者的能力；(3) 基于对裁剪偏差的精确表达式分析，提出偏差校正方法，从而无需权衡偏差与方差，可以进一步减小裁剪阈值、缩小输出空间以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在可信环境下，RPC方法相比现有方法达到了可比或更好的性能，表明其本身就是一个高效的估计器；在不可信环境下，RPC方法对中毒攻击具有显著更强的鲁棒性。同时提供了在所有可能攻击下的估计误差的严格理论保证。

Conclusion: RPC方法通过随机投影与裁剪相结合，并利用精确的偏差校正，在无需偏差-方差权衡的情况下同时实现了高效估计和强鲁棒性，为多物品LDP场景下的中毒攻击防御提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Local differential privacy (LDP) protocols are vulnerable to poisoning attacks. Existing research have proposed efficient defense strategies for single-item users. However, in practice, a user may possess multiple items. The defense against poisoning attacks for multi-item users is challenging, because due to larger output spaces, the adversary can conduct more powerful attacks without being detected. In this paper, we address the robust sparse vector mean estimation problem, in which each user has a vector with $m$ nonzero coordinates. We propose Randomized Projection with Clipping (RPC). Firstly, the server sends a random binary vector to each user. The user then projects its local data on the vector, and clip the value to restrict the attacker's capability. To handle clipping bias, we propose a correction method based on a careful analysis that gives an exact expression of the bias. As a result, bias-variance tradeoff is no longer needed, thus the clipping threshold can be further reduced to shrink the output space and enhance robustness. We provide a rigorous theoretical guarantee of the estimation error under all possible attacks. Numerical experiments show that under trusted environments, our new method achieves comparable or better performance than existing methods, indicating that our method is already an efficient estimator in its own right. Under untrusted environments, our method is also significantly more robust to poisoning attacks.

</details>


### [182] [Generalization and Trade-off in Adversarial Training: An RKHS Perspective via Kernel Integral Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27995)
*Yiling Xie,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）框架下研究对抗训练。通过核积分算子推导了源均匀泛化误差界，揭示了对抗训练在固定多项式谱模型下存在统计精度损失（比极小极大预测基准更慢的平衡泛化率）。该损失源于对抗鲁棒性与观测噪声的交互作用。为此提出两阶段噪声去偏过程，可消除混合项中的噪声贡献，在鲁棒性水平按样本相关阶数选取时，达到极小极大多项式速率（至多差对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练是保护模型免受对抗攻击的强大方法，但其在非参数框架下（RKHS）的泛化行为尚未被充分理解。已有研究缺乏对其统计精度与极小极大最优率之间差距的定量刻画，也无法解释对抗鲁棒性与泛化之间权衡的根本原因。

Method: 本文采用RKHS框架并通过核积分算子研究对抗训练估计器。首先推导源均匀泛化误差界（依赖鲁棒性水平、样本量、源光滑度和核谱）。在固定多项式谱模型下建立匹配的下界，证明平衡泛化率可慢于极小极大预测基准。识别出损失源于鲁棒性项中噪声贡献对逼近速率的拖累（尽管同项降低了估计复杂度）。基于此提出两阶段噪声去偏过程：第一阶段估计噪声贡献，第二阶段从混合鲁棒性项中去除之。

Result: 推导了RKHS对抗训练估计器的源均匀泛化误差界（上界与匹配下界）；证明了对抗训练存在统计精度损失及其成因（噪声与鲁棒性的交互）；提出的两阶段噪声去偏估计器在鲁棒性水平取样本相关阶数时达到极小极大多项式速率（至多差对数因子）；数值实验验证了理论发现并展示了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文在非参数框架下系统刻画了对抗训练的泛化行为，揭示了对抗鲁棒性与泛化之间权衡的深层机制（统计精度损失不可避免），并提供了基于噪声去偏的原理性解决方案。该工作为理解对抗训练的统计极限和设计更优的训练策略提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Adversarial training has emerged as a powerful approach for protecting models against adversarial attacks in a broad range of real-world applications. In this paper, we study adversarial training in the reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) framework through the associated kernel integral operator. We first derive source-uniform generalization error bounds for the RKHS adversarial training estimator in terms of the robustness level, sample size, source smoothness, and kernel spectrum. On a fixed polynomial-spectrum model, we further establish a matching lower bound showing that the optimally balanced generalization rate can be slower than the minimax prediction benchmark. This result reveals a loss of statistical accuracy in adversarial training. Our analysis shows that this loss arises from the interaction between adversarial robustness and observation noise: the noise contribution in the mixed robustness term slows the approximation rate, although the same term reduces the estimation complexity. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage noise-debiased procedure that estimates and removes the noise contribution from the mixed term. The resulting estimator improves the generalization rate and attains the minimax polynomial rate, up to a logarithmic factor, when the robustness level is selected at the stated sample-dependent order. Our results characterize the generalization behavior of adversarial training in a nonparametric framework and provide a new interpretation and a principled solution for the trade-off between adversarial robustness and generalization. Numerical experiments support the theoretical findings and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [183] [On a joint simultaneous learning of relevant feature subsets and subspaces in regression-like problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28080)
*Illia Horenko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出熵最优流形回归（EOMR），扩展了熵最优流形聚类（EOMC），以联合识别非平稳非线性回归问题中的相关特征子集和子空间，在混沌和流体动力学挑战性问题上展现出比现有AI/ML工具（如梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络和TabPFN）更优越的性能，具有线性扩展的迭代和内存复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习工具在处理非平稳、非线性回归问题（尤其是混沌和等离子体物理中的复杂动态系统）时面临巨大挑战，需要一种能够联合识别相关特征子集和子空间的方法，以实现鲁棒且高效的回归学习。

Method: 将熵最优流形聚类（EOMC）扩展为熵最优流形回归（EOMR），通过联合识别特征子集和子空间来实现非平稳非线性回归学习，算法具有线性扩展的迭代和内存复杂度。

Result: 在Lorenz-96系统（F=8和F=12的强混沌与极强混沌状态）以及Hasegawa-Wakatani托卡马克边缘等离子体模型数据上，EOMR相比梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络和TabPFN v.03，预测均方根误差降低数量级，模型复杂度也降低数量级。在Hasegawa-Wakatani案例中，EOMR仅用8个参数的线性因果弱平稳自回归过程就提炼出主导EOF动力学的简洁描述。

Conclusion: EOMR在非平稳非线性动态系统的回归预测中显著优于现有最先进的AI/ML方法，能够以极低的模型复杂度实现高精度预测，特别是能从复杂系统中提炼出极其简洁且有效的物理描述，展示了其在混沌和等离子体物理领域的强大潜力。

Abstract: We extend a recently introduced Entropy-Optimal Manifold Clustering (EOMC) to allow for a joint simultaneous identification of subsets and subspaces of relevant features in nonstationary and nonlinear regression problems. It is shown that the proposed extension - that we coin as Entropy-Optimal Manifold Regression (EOMR) - allows a robust learning with linearly-scaling iteration and memory complexities. EOMR is compared to the most complete set of state-of-the-art tools from the Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) that is available to the author, on the very challenging problems from chaotic and fluid dynamics: (i) on predicting the Lorenz-96 systems dynamics in strongly- and very-strongly chaotic regimes (with forcing parameter being $F=8$ and $F=12$, respectively); and, (ii) on a data from the Hasegawa-Wakatani model on the edge of the tokamak plasma. It is demonstrated that the proposed benchmarks (i) and (ii), indeed, are the very challenging problems for the state of the art ML and AI tools - since both the general-purpose gradient boosted random forests and deep neuronal networks, as well as transformer-based AI tools like TabPFN v.03 (more spezialised for large-dimensional small data learning problems) - result in orders of magnitude inferior root mean squared prediction errors, and orders of magnitude larger model complexities, when compared to the EOMR. For a Hasegawa-Wakatani example, EOMR distills a very simple entropy-optimal and skilful description of the leading Essential Orthogonal Function (EOF) dynamics, given by linear, causal and weakly-stationary autoregressive process described by just 8 parameters.

</details>


### [184] [Uncertainty quantification for trustworthy deep learning: Methods and measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28248)
*H. Martin Gillis,Thomas Trappenberg*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统评述了深度学习中的不确定性量化（UQ）方法，聚焦集成方法与近似贝叶斯方法及其输出汇总度量，提出了将"产生预测分布的方法"与"汇总不确定性的度量"分离的统一框架，并综述了五大方法族、相关领域、评估方法及大语言模型中的不确定性与开放研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在安全关键领域的部署需要可靠的预测置信度估计，但传统架构缺乏原则性的不确定性量化手段。现有UQ综述缺乏对高效集成近似和单次前传方法的深入分析，需要一套统一框架来厘清方法与方法之间的区别和联系。

Method: 论文采用结构化综述方法。首先将方法按五大族组织：贝叶斯神经网络、蒙特卡洛Dropout、深度集成、高效集成近似、末层或单次前传方法；并将相邻工作（证据/先验网络、保形预测、事后校准）与决策时任务（分布外检测、选择性预测）加以定位。随后评述集成多样性理论与不确定性度量的分解，整合评估方法论以确保定性比较具有共同基础。

Result: 论文对各方法族从理论动机、实现方式、经验表现和局限性四个维度进行剖析；在对不确定性度量方面，对比了熵分解与成对散度度量；并对分布式外检测、选择性预测、大语言模型中的不确定性等问题进行了讨论。

Conclusion: 论文指出开放研究方向包括：分类任务的高效认知不确定性度量、末层多样性、分布偏移下的多样性刻画与校准、以及混合架构。强调了将"产生预测分布的方法"与"汇总不确定性的度量"分离统一处理的重要性。

Abstract: The deployment of deep neural networks in safety-critical domains demands reliable estimates of predictive confidence, yet conventional architectures lack principled uncertainty quantification. This survey provides a structured, critical review of methods for Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) in deep learning, scoped to ensemble-based and approximate Bayesian approaches and the measures used to summarize their outputs. Relative to existing UQ surveys, our contribution is depth on efficient ensemble approximations and single-pass methods, and a unified treatment that separates the method producing a predictive distribution from the measure that summarizes its uncertainty. We organize methods into five families: Bayesian neural networks, Monte Carlo Dropout, deep ensembles, efficient ensemble approximations, and last-layer or single-pass approaches. We situate adjacent work on evidential and prior networks, conformal prediction, and post-hoc calibration, together with the decision-time tasks of out-of-distribution detection and selective prediction. For each, we examine theoretical motivation, implementation, empirical performance, and limitations. We then review ensemble diversity theory and uncertainty measures and their decompositions, contrasting the entropy decomposition with pairwise divergence measures, and consolidate evaluation methodology so that our qualitative comparisons share a common basis. We close with a brief treatment of uncertainty in large language models and open research directions, including efficient epistemic measures for classification, last-layer diversity, diversity and calibration under shift, and hybrid architectures.

</details>


### [185] [Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning for Longitudinal Causal Inference with Irregular Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28567)
*Mengfei Ran,Yifeng Shen,Ruijie Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DR-FRL（双重稳健功能表示学习）框架，将不规则的纵向历史数据转化为面向估计目标的潜在状态表示，使基于EIF（高效影响函数）的估计量能够在不规则功能型协变量下保持渐近线性性质，并通过模拟与VitalDB真实数据验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向因果研究中经常记录的是不规则的功能型片段（如实验室值、生理信号、传感器流等），这些数据在不等且信息性时间点上测量。标准双重稳健估计器通常要求标量摘要，而序列学习器优化的预测损失不一定能稳定高效影响函数，导致现有方法难以直接处理这类不规则历史数据。

Method: 提出交叉拟合的DR-FRL工作流：功能和时间编码器将点云与先验历史映射为状态表示；扰动头估计结果、治疗和删失函数；通过EIF定向验证、校准、重叠、尾部与消融诊断评估状态是否支持估计方程。Catoni聚合作为有界影响点估计器单独处理，而非Wald推断的替代。

Result: 若所选状态保留了EIF所需的扰动信息，表示误差进入与普通扰动误差相同的二阶乘积余项，均值估计在显式速率、重叠、校准和稳定性条件下渐近线性。模拟表明在高维功能混淆、信息性测量、弱支持或重尾伪结果下DR-FRL有增益。VitalDB审计显示，该ICU处置端点中标量实验室摘要已携带大部分端点相关信息（负发现）。

Conclusion: DR-FRL为不规则纵向历史数据提供了具有理论保证的双重稳健估计框架，同时通过可解释的诊断工具帮助研究者判断功能表示相对标量摘要的增量价值，其VitalDB应用展示了实际部署中的实用性和批判性发现。

Abstract: Longitudinal causal studies often record histories as irregular functional fragments: laboratory values, physiologic signals, sensor streams, and image-derived summaries measured at unequal and informative times. Standard doubly robust estimators usually require scalar summaries, whereas sequence learners optimize prediction losses that need not stabilize the efficient influence function. We propose Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning (DR-FRL), a cross-fitted workflow that turns irregular histories into estimand-targeted states for observed-history regimes. Functional and temporal encoders map point clouds and prior histories into states; nuisance heads estimate outcome, treatment, and censoring functions; and EIF-targeted validation, calibration, overlap, tail, and ablation diagnostics assess whether the state supports the estimating equation. If the selected state preserves the nuisance information needed by the EIF, representation error enters the same second-order product remainder as ordinary nuisance error, and the mean estimator is asymptotically linear under explicit rate, overlap, calibration, and stability conditions. Catoni aggregation is treated separately as a bounded-influence point estimator, not a replacement for Wald inference. Simulations show gains when functional confounding is high-dimensional, measurement is informative, support is weak, or pseudo-outcomes are heavy-tailed. A VitalDB audit shows that DR-FRL can use irregular laboratory point clouds and deliver a useful negative finding: for this ICU-disposition endpoint, scalar laboratory summaries already carry much endpoint-relevant information.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Entropy-Smooth Convex Optimization Cannot Be Accelerated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27476)
*Jacob M. Aguirre,Dmitrii M. Ostrovskii*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在相对负熵光滑的一类凸函数上，任何一阶方法的收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)，从而说明镜像下降在此类问题上（除对数因子外）是最优的，即加速是不可能的。该结论还被推广到了量子设置（负 von Neumann 熵相对光滑）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在凸优化中，相对光滑性（relative smoothness）框架下是否可以实现加速一直是开放问题。尽管已有工作（Dragomir et al., 2022）证明相对光滑下加速不可能，但其反例使用的代理函数（prox-function）是病态的、与硬实例共同构造的。本文旨在为具有特别良好结构的代理函数（负熵）证明同样的非加速结论，从而更有说服力地回答该问题。

Method: 作者为相对负熵光滑的凸函数类构造了硬实例（hard instance），并证明了在任何一阶方法下收敛率均为Ω(L/T)的下界（要求d=Ω(T²)）。该构造基于标准d-单纯形上的负熵作为代理函数。随后作者将该结果推广到量子设置，即谱面体（Hermitian 半正定单位迹矩阵）上相对负 von Neumann 熵光滑的函数类，得到相同的下界。

Result: 1) 在标准d-单纯形上，对负熵相对L-光滑的凸函数类，任何一阶方法的收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)（当d=Ω(T²)时）；2) 这说明镜像下降在该类函数上是最优的（至多相差对数因子）；3) 与Dragomir等人的病态构造不同，本文针对结构良好的负熵代理函数证明了非加速；4) 相同下界推广至量子设置（负von Neumann熵相对光滑）。

Conclusion: 本文首次为具有良好结构的特定代理函数（负熵）证明了一阶方法的Ω(L/T)收敛率下界，表明在相对光滑框架下加速本质上是不可能的，即便代理函数具有良好的结构。这一结论同时适用于经典和量子设置，为相对光滑优化理论中加速方法的不可能性提供了更强、更具实际意义的证据。

Abstract: We prove an $Ω(L/T)$ lower bound for the convergence rate of minimization in the class of functions that are convex and $L$-smooth relative to negative entropy on the standard $d$-simplex, valid for every first-order method when $d = Ω(T^2)$. In particular, this shows that mirror descent is optimal up to a logarithmic factor in this class. This may be surprising due to the fact that accelerated methods are readily available under the assumption of smoothness in $\ell_1$-norm. While Dragomir et al. (Mathematical Programming, 2022) have already showed that acceleration might be impossible under relative smoothness, their prox-function is pathological and constructed together with the hard instance. In contrast, we show non-acceleration for a specific prox-function with particularly favorable structure. We also extend the result to the quantum setting, proving the same lower bound in the class of functions $L$-smooth relative to negative von Neumann entropy on the spectrahedron of $d \times d$ Hermitian positive-semidefinite matrices with unit trace.

</details>


### [187] [Branching out: Prognostics-Based Replacement Policies for Series Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27899)
*Daniel Koutas,Daniel Straub*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预后信息的混合规划方法，用于制定预测性维护策略。该方法仅需少量参数即可定义策略，可通过理论或数据优化确定参数，在预防性更换和预防性订货两个决策场景中性能媲美优化基准策略，且更高效、对过拟合更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测性维护策略往往需要大量参数或过于依赖复杂优化，在实际应用中效率低且容易过拟合，需要一个既简洁又具有竞争力的策略制定方法。

Method: 提出一种混合规划方法，融合了可用决策选项、预后模型提供的未来系统状态信息以及更新-报酬过程的成本，制定由少数参数定义的维护策略。参数可通过理论推导或基于失效数据的优化确定。

Result: 在预防性更换和预防性订货两个独立的预测性维护决策场景中进行了数值验证，结果表明所提出的策略性能与优化基准策略接近，同时在效率和对过拟合的鲁棒性方面显著更优。

Conclusion: 该混合规划方法为预测性维护策略提供了一种兼顾性能、简洁性和鲁棒性的新途径，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid planning method for deriving prognostics-based predictive maintenance policies. The method accounts for the available decision options, the information on the future state of the system provided by a prognostic model, and the costs of the underlying renewal-reward process. It results in policies defined by only a few parameters, which can be determined based on theoretical considerations or by optimization from run-to-failure data. We demonstrate the potential of the method in two separate predictive maintenance decision settings: preventive replacement and preventive ordering. Numerical investigations show that the derived policies rival the performance of optimized benchmark policies, while being significantly more efficient and robust against overfitting.

</details>


### [188] [The Existence and Stability of Generalized Multi-Source Weber Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27900)
*V. S. T. Long,N. M. Nam,T. H. Tai,C. Tammer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在最小时间函数框架下研究具有集值目标的广义多源Weber问题，建立了全局与局部最优解的存在性，分析了解集的定性性质（闭性、紧性、有界性/无界性条件），推导了目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的Lipschitz连续性，并引入全局与局部解映射，从集值分析视角研究了其稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Weber问题（也称Fermat-Weber问题或设施选址问题）通常假设目标为单点集，但在实际应用中（如区域覆盖选址、传感器部署等），目标往往是不确定或集合形式的。本文将问题推广到集值目标的情形，并在最小时间函数框架下进行系统研究，以提供更一般、更实用的理论支撑。

Method: 本文采用最小时间函数作为核心分析工具，分四步展开：首先利用变分分析方法建立全局与局部最优解的存在性定理；其次通过集值分析技术刻画解集的闭性、紧性及有界性/无界性条件；再次运用Lipschitz分析推导目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的连续性估计；最后引入全局与局部解映射，利用集值映射的稳定性理论（如上半连续、下半连续等性质）进行灵敏度分析。

Result: 获得了以下主要结果：（1）全局与局部最优解的存在性定理；（2）解集的闭性、紧性以及有界性/无界性的充要或充分条件；（3）目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的显式Lipschitz连续性界；（4）全局与局部解映射的稳定性性质刻画。

Conclusion: 本文为广义多源Weber问题提供了完整的定量与定性灵敏度分析框架，在最小时间函数设定下系统揭示了最优解结构、解集几何性质以及解映射的稳定性规律，为后续在不确定环境下的设施选址问题研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the generalized multi-source Weber problem with set-valued targets in the framework of minimal time functions. We first establish the existence of global and local optimal solutions and investigate several qualitative properties of the corresponding solution sets, including closedness, compactness, and conditions ensuring boundedness or unboundedness. Next, we derive explicit Lipschitz continuity properties for the objective function and the associated optimal value function with respect to perturbations of the target sets. We then introduce the global and local solution mappings and study their stability properties from the viewpoint of set-valued analysis. These results provide a quantitative and qualitative sensitivity analysis for the generalized multi-source Weber problem in the setting of minimal time functions.

</details>


### [189] [OptGraph: Large Language Models Enhanced Evolutionary Optimization Via Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27918)
*Xianchao Xiu,Jianhao Li,Huangyue Chen,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: OptGraph是首个引入图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）的优化智能体工作流，通过构建可复用的类型化图经验，利用图邻域信息增强检索上下文，并支持自适应知识更新，在基准测试上比现有最先进的基于提示的自动优化框架平均精确准确率高出8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动化进化优化方法存在三个局限：模式复用能力不足、缺乏错误感知的精炼机制、跨多样任务的检索鲁棒性差，亟需一种更系统化的知识组织与检索方式。

Method: OptGraph首先将可复用经验构建为类型化图，捕捉建模模式、问题形式化、实现细节和错误修正之间的关系；推理阶段利用图邻域信息丰富检索知识，提供结构化上下文以改进建模、验证和迭代精炼；同时支持自适应知识更新，在执行轨迹和验证反馈中提炼可复用图知识，无需进行LLM参数微调。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，OptGraph相比最先进的基于提示的自动化优化框架，平均精确准确率提升了8.9%。

Conclusion: OptGraph通过图结构化的知识表示与检索增强，有效解决了现有LLM驱动优化方法在模式复用、错误感知精炼和检索鲁棒性方面的不足，验证了GraphRAG在自动化进化优化中的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for automated evolutionary optimization, but existing methods remain limited in pattern reuse, error-aware refinement, and retrieval robustness across diverse tasks. To address these limitations, we propose OptGraph, the first optimization agentic workflow that introduces graph retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG). Specifically, OptGraph first constructs reusable experience as a typed graph, capturing the relationships among modeling patterns, problem formalization, implementation details, and error corrections. In the inference stage, OptGraph leverages graph neighborhood information to enrich retrieved knowledge, providing structured context to improve modeling, verification, and iterative refinement. Moreover, OptGraph supports adaptive knowledge updates, enabling the distillation of execution traces and verification feedback into reusable graph knowledge without ndertaking LLM parameter tuning. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that our proposed OptGraph achieves an average exact accuracy 8.9% higher than the state-of-the-art prompt-based automated optimization frameworks. Our code has been made available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/OptGraph.

</details>


### [190] [Global exponential turnpike properties for optimal control of the viscous Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28042)
*Emmanuel Trélat,Xingwu Zeng,Can Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对由一维黏性Burgers方程(带局部内部控制)描述的二次最优跟踪问题,建立了全局指数转折(turnpike)性质。对于任意初始数据,当周期跟踪目标足够小时,有限时域最优解趋近于唯一的最优周期稳态;零目标情形则在原点处给出全局稳态转折,且无需对初始数据作小性假设。这是黏性Burgers方程的首个全局指数转折结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burgers方程是最具代表性的非线性偏微分方程之一,广泛出现在流体力学、交通流等领域。虽然线性/抛物线系统的转折性质已被深入研究,但非线性Burgers方程的全局转折性质此前尚未有结果,因此填补这一理论空白是该工作的核心动机。

Method: 证明方法分两步:(1)利用严格凸性和周期Riccati理论建立局部指数转折性质;(2)借助抛物耗散论证获得与时间域无关的吸收时间,将局部结果提升为全局结果。

Result: 得到两个全局指数转折结果:一是对足够小的周期跟踪目标,任意初始数据的有限时域最优解指数逼近唯一最优周期稳态;二是零目标情形下,全局稳态转折于原点成立,且无需对初始数据作小性假设。

Conclusion: 本文首次为黏性Burgers方程建立了全局指数转折性质,证明了该非线性系统在长时间最优控制中的稳态行为具有全局吸引性,为非线性PDE最优控制的转折理论研究提供了新的途径。

Abstract: We establish global exponential turnpike properties for quadratic optimal tracking problems governed by the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with localized internal control. For every initial datum, finite-horizon optimal solutions approach the unique optimal periodic regime when the periodic tracking target is sufficiently small; the zero-target case yields a global steady turnpike at the origin, with no smallness assumption on the initial datum. To our knowledge, these are the first global exponential turnpike results for the viscous Burgers equation. The proof combines a local exponential turnpike, obtained through strict convexity and periodic Riccati theory, with a parabolic dissipation argument that provides an absorbing time independent of the horizon.

</details>


### [191] [Multi-Asset Utility Maximization with Jump Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28051)
*Sigui Brice Dro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多维布朗运动与独立齐次泊松随机测度驱动的风险资产下的投资组合效用最大化问题，允许策略包含跳跃信号。采用鞅最优性原理推导含跳跃的倒向随机微分方程（BSDE），证明其解的存在唯一性，讨论异质跳跃信号情况，并给出高斯信号下对数效用情形的数值示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已有的一维指数效用函数框架推广到更一般的多维跳扩散模型，并处理投资者收到异质跳跃信号的情形，以更贴近实际金融市场中存在跳跃风险和信号异质性的场景。

Method: 首先将组合动态表示为半鞅过程；随后运用鞅最优性原理推导含跳跃的BSDE，通过其解刻画价值函数与最优策略；接着证明该BSDE解的存在唯一性；最后以高斯信号下对数效用情形进行数值说明。

Result: 成功建立了多维跳扩散模型下投资组合效用最大化问题的BSDE框架，证明了含跳跃BSDE解的存在唯一性，处理了异质跳跃信号的特殊情形，并通过数值实验验证了对数效用情形下的方法有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为多维跳扩散市场中包含跳跃信号的投资组合优化问题提供了完整的理论框架和数值验证，拓展了已有单维指数效用结果，为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study portfolio utility maximization problem in a setting where the risky asset is driven by a multidimensional Brownian motion and an independent homogeneous Poisson random measure, and where strategies may incorporate jump signals. Following the same approach as in the one-dimensional case for the exponential utility function [17], we first represent the portfolio dynamics as semimartingale processes. We then use martingale optimality principle to derive the corresponding backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) with jumps to characterize both value function and an optimal strategy in terms of its solution. We subsequently prove the existence and uniqueness of the solution to the related BSDE with jumps. We also address the idiosyncratic case i.e where the investor receive heterogeneous jump signals. Finally, we provide numerical illustrations with Gaussian signals for the logarithmic case.

</details>


### [192] [On Leader Selection for Strong Structural Controllability in Matrix-Weighted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28168)
*Lanhao Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个严格的数学框架来解决矩阵加权网络中最小领导者集选择以保障强结构可控性（SSC）这一NP-hard逆合成问题，将结构不可控归因于维度特定的可达性隔离和拓扑对称等价，并设计两阶段综合方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在矩阵加权网络中，选择最小领导者集来保证强结构可控性（SSC）的逆合成问题是一个尚未解决的NP-hard难题，亟需一个严谨的数学框架来系统性地解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出两阶段综合方法：第一阶段为可达性先决条件，用于识别结构根节点；第二阶段包含三种不同的对称破缺算法——贪心Weisfeiler-Lehman选择、子模边界最大化和分区熵最大化。同时提供数学证明以保证对不变子空间和结构膨胀的免疫性。

Result: 通过证明结构不可控性仅源于维度特定的可达性隔离和拓扑对称等价，并借助三种对称破缺算法及数学证明，在多样化拓扑上进行了大量数值评估，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过严格的数学证明和数值验证，为矩阵加权网络中的SSC最小领导者集选择问题提供了系统性解决方案，保证了免疫不变子空间和结构膨胀的能力。

Abstract: The inverse synthesis problem of selecting a minimal leader set to guarantee strong structural controllability (SSC) in matrix-weighted networks remains an unresolved NP-hard challenge. This paper proposes a rigorous mathematical framework to solve this. We prove that structural uncontrollability stems exclusively from dimension-specific reachability isolation and topological symmetry equivalence. To overcome these bottlenecks, we formulate a two-phase synthesis: a reachability prerequisite to identify structural roots, followed by three distinct symmetry-breaking algorithms (Greedy Weisfeiler-Lehman Selection, Submodular Bound Maximization, and Partition Entropy Maximization). Mathematical proofs guarantee immunity to invariant subspaces and structural dilation, validated by extensive numerical evaluations across diverse topologies.

</details>


### [193] [The optimality of an (s, S) hiring policy on a workforce planning problem with fixed recruitment costs and binomial turnover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28171)
*Zhen Chen,Roberto Rossi,Belen Martin-Barragan,S. Armagan Tarim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域的人力规划问题，其中每期员工流失服从二项分布，其参数取决于雇佣后的劳动力水平。模型包含固定招聘成本，目标是最小化期望总成本（包括招聘、工资和短缺成本）。作者引入了Binomial-K-convexity概念证明Bellman函数中K-凸性在二项转移下得以保持，并证明最优招聘策略呈现(s,S)型结构。此外，提出分段逼近方法将问题转化为混合整数线性规划(MILP)，数值实验表明方法计算快速且最优性间隙小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的劳动力规划问题中，员工流失率并非固定不变，而是与当前劳动力规模相关（即决策依赖的转移概率），这使得标准动态规划中的凸性分析工具不再适用。同时，固定招聘成本的存在导致成本函数非凸，需要更精细的凸性框架来刻画最优策略结构并设计有效的求解算法。

Method: 建立了随机动态规划模型，其中每期流失人数服从二项分布且参数依赖雇佣后的劳动力水平。引入Binomial-K-凸性的新概念，用于证明Bellman方程中K-凸性在二项转移下的保持性，从而推导出最优策略为(s,S)型。进一步开发分段线性逼近方法，将原问题转化为MILP进行求解并计算各期(s,S)参数。

Result: 证明了最优招聘策略具有(s,S)型结构：当劳动力水平低于阈值s时招聘至S水平，否则不招聘。所提出的MILP方法能够高效计算各期参数，数值实验验证了方法的计算速度和小最优性间隙。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典的(s,S)策略理论推广到决策依赖的二项流失情形，通过引入新的凸性工具（Binomial-K-凸性）解决了理论上的关键难点，并提供了可计算的MILP求解框架，为实际劳动力规划问题提供了理论保障和高效算法。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon workforce planning problem in which staff turnover in each period follows a binomial distribution whose parameters depend on the post-hiring workforce level. The model incorporates a fixed hiring cost that is incurred whenever recruitment occurs, regardless of the number of employees hired. The objective is to minimise the expected total cost, including recruitment, salary, and shortage costs, where deviations below period-specific staffing requirements are penalised. To analyse this stochastic dynamic programme with decision-dependent transition probabilities, we establish the discrete convexity of the variable single-period cost (the sum of expected salary and penalty costs) and the K-convexity of the expected total cost. Specifically, we introduce the concept of Binomial-K-convexity to facilitate the proof that K-convexity is preserved under Binomial propagation in the Bellman function. We then show that the optimal hiring policy exhibits an (s, S)-type structure: when the workforce level in a given period falls below a threshold s, staff are hired up to level S; otherwise, no hiring occurs. Furthermore, we develop a piecewise approximation approach that yields a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation for solving the problem and computing the (s, S) parameters for each period. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves fast computation with small optimality gaps.

</details>


### [194] [No-gap second-order conditions for optimization problems involving transport distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28264)
*Nicolas Borchard,Christian Meyer,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究度量空间中的优化问题，以到给定先验测度的输运距离作为正则项，利用弱-*二阶次微分推导无间隙型二阶最优性条件，并在平滑目标和Kantorovich势的额外假设下证明与二次增长的等价性，最后将结果应用于度量空间中的最优控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在度量空间中的优化问题中，引入输运距离作为正则项后，需要建立严谨的二阶最优性条件。现有理论可能无法直接刻画这类问题的二阶行为，因此需要借助弱-*二阶次微分理论来推导无间隙型的二阶必要条件与充分条件。

Method: 采用弱-*二阶次微分理论推导无间隙型二阶最优性条件；计算弱-*二阶次微分的具体表达式；证明弱-*上微分可微性（weak-* epidifferentiability）；在目标函数光滑部分和Kantorovich势的额外假设下建立与二次增长的等价性。

Result: 成功推导了以输运距离为正则项的度量空间优化问题的无间隙型二阶最优性条件，证明了在适当假设下这些条件与二次增长性质等价，并给出了弱-*二阶次微分的显式计算及弱-*上微分可微性的证明，且结果被成功应用于度量空间中的最优控制问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过弱-*二阶次微分理论为含输运距离正则项的度量空间优化问题建立了完整的二阶最优性条件框架，理论结果对度量空间中的最优控制问题具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems in the space of measures. As a regularization term, the problem includes the transport distance to a given prior measure. For the derivation of second-order optimality conditions of no-gap type, the theory of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives is used which will lead to an equivalence with quadratic growth under additional assumptions on the smooth part of the objective and on the Kantorovich potential, i.e., the solution of the dual transport problem. Further, the weak-$\star$ second subderivative is calculated and weak-$\star$ epidifferentiability is proven. Finally, the results are applied to optimal control problems in measure space.

</details>


### [195] [Exact and Approximate Solvability of Systems Involving Proximity Operators in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28343)
*Mark Allien D. Roble*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究实Hilbert空间中有限个正常下半连续凸函数的邻近算子方程系统，建立其精确解和近似解存在唯一的充要条件，并引入逆邻近性质（IPP）作为逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）的推广，应用于可行性问题和信号恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典投影问题可视为邻近算子方程的特例，研究将投影问题推广到更一般的邻近算子系统，为优化与逆问题提供统一的理论框架。

Method: 通过分析有限凸函数的邻近算子方程系统的结构，建立解的存在性与唯一性的充分必要条件，提出逆邻近性质（IPP）并给出多种刻画，将其与逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）相联系。

Result: 给出了该系统精确解存在唯一的充要条件，建立了近似解存在性的判据，推导了IPP的多种等价刻画，并在可行性问题和信号恢复问题中验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将投影问题的理论推广至更一般的邻近算子框架，为Hilbert空间中的优化和逆问题提供了新的分析工具与应用途径。

Abstract: Let $\HH$ be a real Hilbert space and let $(f_i)_{i\in I}$ be a finite family of proper, lower semicontinuous, and convex functions on $\HH$. This study investigates the existence and uniqueness of exact solutions to systems involving proximity operators of the form: \
  $(\forall i\in I)\ \pr{f_i}(x)=p_i,$
  where $(p_i)_{i\in I}$ is a prescribed collection of proximal points in $\HH$, which naturally generalize classical projection problems. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of approximate solutions to such systems. Moreover, we introduce and derive several characterizations of the inverse proximal property (IPP), as a generalization of the inverse best approximation property (IBAP). Applications of the obtained results are presented in the contexts of a feasibility problem and signal recovery problem, demonstrating the relevance of the proposed framework to optimization and inverse problems in Hilbert spaces.

</details>


### [196] [Beyond Hand-Derived Inequalities: Decision Diagrams for Cut Generation in Binary Polynomial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28511)
*Martin Cooper,Margarita Castro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于决策图(DD)的割平面生成方法，用于二元多项式优化(BPO)中的多线性集割分离。该方法对任意选定的支撑集，可在局部多线性多面体中分离出定义面的割并提升回原问题，相比传统手推割（如two-links、flowers、odd β-cycles）更具一般性。DD编码还带来扩展公式、基于有效反链的宽度刻画（揭示新的多项式可解超图谱类）以及割的面对性证明。实验表明该方法在根节点间隙闭合和加速分支定界上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强割（两链接、花、奇β环等）是针对固定支撑模式手工推导的，缺乏通用性和系统性。作者希望提出一种自动化方法，对任意支撑集都能分离多线性多面体的强割，从而克服手工推导的局限并提升BPO的求解效率。

Method: (1) 提出基于决策图(DD)的割分离框架：对任意支撑，先在局部多线性多面体中分离定义面的割，再提升回原问题；(2) 设计新型紧凑DD编码，基于递归公式仅表示顶点变量，超边变量隐式编码在状态表示中；(3) 从DD编码中推导出多线性多面体的扩展公式、基于有效反链的宽度刻画（发现新的多项式可解超图谱类）以及割的面对性证明；(4) 提出三种支撑选择策略（复用、扩展、分割局部结构为分区超图）；(5) 通过大规模实验验证方法有效性。

Result: 实验结果显示，DD-based方法在根节点能实现更大的间隙闭合（gap closure），且使用更少的割数量，相比现有程序显著加速了分支定界过程。同时发现了一个新的多项式可解超图谱类。

Conclusion: 基于决策图的割生成方法为二元多项式优化的强割生成提供了一个通用的自动化框架，兼具理论上的面割保证与实验上的求解效率优势，为BPO问题提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: We study cutting-plane generation for binary polynomial optimization (BPO), whose feasible region is the multilinear set of a hypergraph. Strong inequalities for this set---such as two-links, flowers, and odd $β$-cycles---are classically hand-derived for fixed support patterns. Instead, we propose a decision-diagram (DD) approach: for any chosen support, it separates a facet-defining cut in the local multilinear polytope and lifts it back to the original problem. We utilize a novel compact DD encoding based on a recursive formulation that represents only the vertex variables and implicitly encodes the hyperedge variables inside the state representation. From this DD encoding, we also obtain: (i) an extended formulation of the multilinear polytope, (ii) a width characterization via valid antichains that uncovers a new polynomially solvable class of hypergraphs, and (iii) a certificate for the facetness of the generated cuts. We explore three different support strategies for cut generation that reuse, expand, or partition local structures into section hypergraphs. Our empirical results show that our DD-based methodologies achieve a larger gap closure at the root node with fewer cuts than existing procedures and, in turn, markedly accelerate branch-and-bound procedures.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [A sub-grid-scale model for polydisperse bubbly flows with heat and mass transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27426)
*Anand Radhakrishnan,Spencer H. Bryngelson*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文针对多分散气泡流的系综平均模型，提出了一种条件双曲求积方法，用恒定传递方程描述气泡压力和蒸气质量，避免使用省略壁面传热传质的多方关系式闭包。该方法无需在输运矩组中增加混合压力或蒸气质量矩，通过二次条件反演在每个平衡半径区间生成四个半径-径向速度联合节点。在MFC中实现后，用蒙特卡洛和三维气泡屏幕算例验证了精度（Euler-Euler与Euler-Lagrange平均压力相对均方根误差1.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多分散气泡流的系综平均模型需要气泡群体的演化统计量。现有基于矩的求积方法以多方关系式闭包气泡压力，该关系式忽略了气泡壁处的传热与传质，导致在考虑这些物理过程时闭包不准确，且压力会出现高频振荡。

Method: 提出恒定传递方程来描述气泡压力和蒸气质量，并将其嵌入条件双曲求积方法中。通过二次条件反演，在每个平衡半径区间内生成四个半径-径向速度联合节点；气泡压力和蒸气质量在各节点上推进，由此闭合系综平均流动方程，而无需向输运矩集添加混合压力或蒸气质量矩。模型在MFC（多相流代码）中实现。

Result: 蒙特卡洛计算验证了谐波强迫群体下求积节点及平均气泡变量的演化正确性。气泡屏幕算例量化了平衡半径离散化和初始分布变化时的闭包误差。恒定传递计算在所述条件下未出现多方闭包中观察到的高频压力振荡。三维气泡屏幕计算中，Euler-Euler中心压力与40个体积平均Euler-Lagrange实现均值的相对均方根误差为1.5%。

Conclusion: 恒定传递闭包方案在避免颗粒壁传热传质被忽略所造成误差的同时，消除了高频压力振荡，且在不增加输运矩维数的情况下实现了与Euler-Lagrange参考解良好一致的结果，为多分散气泡流的工程模拟提供了更准确的基于矩的求解框架。

Abstract: Ensemble-averaged models of polydisperse bubbly flows require statistics of the evolving bubble population. Prior quadrature-based moment formulations close bubble pressure with a polytropic relation that omits heat and mass transfer at the bubble wall. We formulate constant-transfer equations for bubble pressure and vapor mass within a conditional hyperbolic quadrature method. Second-order conditional inversion produces four joint radius--radial-velocity nodes per equilibrium-radius bin. Bubble pressure and vapor mass are advanced at each node. The node values close the ensemble-averaged flow equations without adding mixed pressure or vapor-mass moments to the transported moment set. The model is implemented in MFC. Monte Carlo calculations verify the evolution of quadrature nodes and the mean bubble variables for a harmonically forced population. Bubble-screen calculations quantify closure error as the equilibrium radius is discretized and the initial distributions vary. The constant-transfer calculation does not exhibit the high-frequency pressure oscillations observed with the polytropic closure under the conditions considered. 3D bubble-screen calculations give a 1.5% relative root-mean-square error between the Euler--Euler center pressure and the mean of 40 volume-averaged Euler--Lagrange realizations.

</details>


### [198] [Kinetic Linear Stability Theory for High-Speed Compressible Flows: A High Performance Computing Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27440)
*Irmak Taylan Karpuzcu,Deborah Levin,Vassilis Theofilis*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对一维正激波的动理学线性稳定性理论（kLST），通过线性化Boltzmann-BGK方程，在速度分布函数（VDF）层面而非连续介质闭合系统上进行稳定性分析，并开发了并行SLEPc/PETSc大规模特征值求解框架，成功完成了马赫数4.0激波（28万未知量）的稳定性谱计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典激波稳定性分析依赖Navier-Stokes或矩闭合模型，无法保留激波内部的双峰速度分布等非平衡效应。当激波内存在有限厚度、高梯度的区域时，连续介质假设失效，需要一种能够保留微观速度分布非平衡特征的稳定性理论。

Method: 将Boltzmann-BGK方程关于动理学BE-BGK基流线性化，建立kLST稳定性算子。扰动以约化分布函数形式给出，宏观场通过速度空间矩恢复。针对大规模高马赫数矩阵，开发并行SLEPc/PETSc基础设施：中等规模使用shift-and-invert Arnoldi配合MUMPS LU，最大规模使用Jacobi-Davidson配合块Jacobi ILU预处理。

Result: 在连续介质极限下验证于可压缩Couette特征值基准；对氩气激波在M∞=1.2、3.0、4.0下计算。低马赫数下谱与连续结果一致；高马赫数下非平衡VDF谱向更不稳定区域偏移。成功完成M∞=4.0、28万未知量的kLST计算，是迄今公开报道的最高马赫数孤立有限厚度激波层动理学线性稳定性计算。

Conclusion: 动理学效应会使激波稳定性谱向更不稳定方向偏移，即使宏观剖面看似良好分辨，连续介质预测仍可能遗漏重要的稳定性变化；直接求解器因耦合空间/微观速度稀疏性导致的严重LU填充而内存受限，需采用JD类迭代方法处理最大规模问题。

Abstract: Shock waves in high-speed compressible flows contain finite-thickness, high-gradient regions where the continuum assumption becomes questionable and translational non-equilibrium arises, including non-Maxwellian micro-velocity distributions. Classical shock stability analyses rely on Navier-Stokes or moment closures and cannot retain bi-modal velocity distributions inside the shock. We develop and apply, for the first time, a kinetic linear stability theory (kLST) for one-dimensional normal shocks by linearizing the Boltzmann-BGK equation about kinetic BE-BGK base flows. Perturbations are posed in reduced distribution functions, with macroscopic fields recovered by velocity-space moments, so the stability operator acts on the VDF rather than a closed continuum system.
  Verified against compressible Couette eigenvalue benchmarks near continuum, the framework is applied to argon shocks at $M_\infty=1.2$, $3.0$, and $4.0$. At low Mach number, where BE-BGK and Gilbarg-Paolucci profiles nearly coincide, the spectra recover stable continuous branches. At higher Mach number, comparing Maxwellian and non-equilibrium VDF-based eigenspectra shows that kinetic effects shift the spectrum toward less stable regions, so continuum predictions can miss important changes even when macroscopic profiles appear well resolved.
  For large high-Mach matrices--$O(10^5)$ unknowns and up to billions of nonzeros--we develop a parallel SLEPc/PETSc infrastructure using shift-and-invert Arnoldi with MUMPS LU for moderate sizes and Jacobi-Davidson (JD) with block-Jacobi ILU for the largest systems. Coupled spatial/micro-velocity sparsity causes severe LU fill-in, making direct solvers memory-limited and motivating JD. We compute kLST spectra for an $M_\infty=4.0$ shock with 281088 unknowns, to our knowledge the highest-Mach kinetic linear stability calculation reported for isolated finite-thickness shock layers.

</details>


### [199] [Role of gravity on preferential clustering of microparticles in unsteady wake flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27459)
*Siddhi Arya,Partha S. Goswami*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过直接数值模拟研究了垂直无界通道流中圆柱绕流携带颗粒的两相流动，重点考察了雷诺数、颗粒Stokes数、颗粒载荷和Froude数对颗粒优先聚集的影响，发现无量纲沉降速度St/Fr²是控制空隙形态、归一化空隙面积和Voronoi单元面积概率分布的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解钝体绕流尾迹中颗粒的优先聚集行为对于诸多工程应用（如气固分离、污染物扩散等）至关重要，但颗粒惯性、重力沉降与尾迹涡动力学三者之间的耦合作用机制尚不清晰，需要系统量化研究。

Method: 采用直接数值模拟方法，对Re=100和200的圆柱绕流携带颗粒流动进行参数化研究，考察不同Stokes数、颗粒载荷和Froude数的影响；结合Voronoi分析和局部Q值方法，量化颗粒聚集与空隙结构，分析重力与惯性对颗粒分布的联合效应。

Result: 发现颗粒在非均匀尾迹流场中形成相干空隙和聚集簇，空隙形状随重力作用从叶状过渡为蛇状并最终趋于近垂直结构；上游区域形成弓形激波状颗粒结构；St/Fr²被识别为控制空隙形态演化和颗粒空间分布统计特征的主导无量纲参数。

Conclusion: 尾迹动力学、颗粒惯性和重力共同主导钝体绕流尾迹中的优先聚集行为，无量纲沉降速度St/Fr²可作为统一描述颗粒聚集与空隙演化的控制参数。

Abstract: Direct numerical simulations are carried out for particle-laden flow over the cylinder to investigate preferential clustering of particles in an unbounded vertical channel flow. The flow is examined at Reynolds numbers Re=100 and 200 for varying particle Stokes number, particle loadings and Froude numbers to quantify the combined influence of particle inertia and gravitational settling on particle motion. The unladen flow exhibits the classical vortex shedding pattern observed in flow over bluff bodies at both Reynolds numbers. Reynolds number dependent wake width, wake recovery, and velocity-deficit evolution are observed, characterizing the coherent flow structures that govern particle dynamics. In particle-laden unsteady wake flows, the non-uniform particle distribution leads to formation of coherent voids and clusters, whose shape are directly correlated with background flow dynamics. Gravity modifies particle-fluid interaction, which leads to an increase in slip velocity, weakens vortex-induced particle clustering and promotes them to travel through vortices, resulting in a transition of the void shape from individual leaf-like structure to snake-like void zone and eventually into a nearly vertical void structure. In upstream region infront of the cylinder, inertial particles form a bow-shock-like structure whose extent increases with increase in Stokes number and finite Froude conditions. Voronoi based analysis combined with local Q values is used to investigate effect of gravity and inertia on particle distribution. The dimensionless settling velocity, St/Fr^2, is identified as the governing parameter controlling the evolution of void shape, normalized void cell area and the probability distribution of Voronoi cell areas. The effect of wake dynamics, particle inertia, and gravity is reported to jointly govern preferential clustering in bluff-body wakes.

</details>


### [200] [Gaussian non relativistic spontaneusly stochastic hydrodynamica](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27540)
*David Montenegro,Giorgio Torrieri*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯协变流体动力学的非相对论极限，论证不可压缩条件提供了与相对论流体动力学匹配的额外对称性，但不可压缩性在"微观"尺度上必须被打破。作者针对该尺度发展了均值和涨落的重整化群方程，揭示统计力学涨落如何作为宏观反作用自然产生自发随机性，并表现出异常耗散和"奇异解"等特征（作为重整化群反项）。论文从现象学角度讨论流体动力学适用范围的极限，并探讨物理学如何为湍流相关的数学问题提供启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在弥合流体动力学与统计力学之间的尺度鸿沟，理解不可压缩流体动力学在微观尺度失效的机制，并探索物理学视角如何帮助解决与湍流相关的深层数学问题（如奇异解、异常耗散等）。

Method: 采用非相对论极限分析高斯协变流体动力学，通过引入"微观"尺度（不可压缩性在该尺度失效），发展针对该尺度的重整化群方程，分别处理均值和涨落，将统计力学涨落视为宏观动力学中的反作用项。

Result: 得到包含自发随机性（作为统计力学涨落的宏观反作用）、异常耗散和"奇异解"（作为重整化群反项）的自洽动力学框架，为流体动力学与统计力学之间的过渡提供了新的理论描述。

Conclusion: 流体动力学的适用范围受限于不可压缩性成立的尺度；重整化群方法能够系统化描述统计力学涨落的宏观效应，同时为湍流中的数学难题（如奇异解的存在性）提供了物理层面的解释路径。

Abstract: We study the non-relativistic limit of Gaussian covariant hydrodynamics [1]. We argue that the condition of incompressibility provides additional symmetries matching relativistic hydrodynamics but incompressibility must break down at a ``microscopic`` scale. We then develop the renormalization group equations for average and fluctuations w.r.t. that scale, to understand its effect on flows at intermolecular distances where hydrodynamics gives way to statistical mechanics. The resulting dynamics naturally incorporates spontaneous stochasticity as a macroscopic back reaction of statistical mechanics fluctuations, as well as features reminiscent of anomalous dissipation and ``wild solutions`` as renormalization group counterterms. We frame these considerations into both a phenomenological discussion of the limits of applicability of fluid dynamics, and a discussion of where physics might shed some light on the mathematical issues associated with turbulence.

</details>


### [201] [Modified Dynamic Mixed Subgrid-scale Models for Geophysical Flows: Forced Two-Dimensional and $β$-plane Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27567)
*Anantha Narayanan Suresh Babu,Akhil Sadam,Pierre F. J. Lermusiaux*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文针对LES中亚网格尺度(SGS)模型中动态混合模型(DMM)的结构性与耗散性组分失衡问题，提出了一种基于修正Gram框架的参数化模型族，包含全耦合、顺序和全解耦三种变体。研究发现，尽管结构主导模型在前验测试中表现优异，但在后验测试中会出现噪声伪影和高波数谱偏差；而先确定功能组分再以结构组分修正的顺序DMM模型能够在保持前验结构精度的同时改善后验涡度场、谱和域平均诊断量，实现瞬时结构保真与长期精度之间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典动态混合模型(DMM)通过最小二乘估计同时优化功能性和结构性闭合的系数，但该方法往往被结构性组分主导，导致功能组分的耗散正则化作用受限。作者旨在解决这一平衡问题，通过开发新的框架来构建可调节结构-功能平衡的DMM变体。

Method: 开发基于修正Gram框架的参数化模型族，构建三类DMM变体：全耦合(fully-coupled)、顺序(sequential)和全解耦(fully-decoupled)动态混合模型。在理想化的二维和β-平面湍流框架下，使用Leith模型和四阶非线性梯度模型进行评估，分别进行前验(a priori)和后验(a posteriori)测试，并分析谱SGS能量与涡度拟能传递。

Result: 前验测试中，结构主导模型与理想SGS强迫高度一致并能准确再现局部SGS能量交换（包括反向散射）；但后验测试中结构主导模型出现噪声伪影和高波数谱偏差，表明净耗散不足。顺序DMM（先确定功能组分再被结构组分修正）保留了大部分前验结构精度，同时改善了后验涡度场、谱和域平均诊断量，在大于强迫尺度的尺度上允许反向散射，在较小尺度上增强耗散。

Conclusion: 顺序DMM能够有效地平衡瞬时结构保真与长期计算精度之间的权衡，为LES中SGS模型的设计提供了改进方案。通过调整结构-功能组分的确定顺序和耦合方式，可以克服经典DMM中结构性组分主导所带来的耗散不足问题。

Abstract: Subgrid-scale (SGS) models for large-eddy simulations (LES) of geophysical turbulence typically need to balance dissipative regularization with backscatter, the upscale transfer of energy from unresolved to resolved scales. Dynamic mixed models (DMMs) combine functional eddy viscosity and structural closures through dynamically estimated coefficients that are least-squares optimal with respect to the Germano identity error (GIE). We show that this classical DMM least-squares estimation can be dominated by the structural component, thereby limiting the functional component's dissipative regularizing role. To address this limitation, we develop a modified Gram-based framework to construct a novel parametric family of fully-coupled, sequential, and fully-decoupled DMMs with tunable structural-functional balance. We evaluate the resulting closures using an idealized forced two-dimensional and $β$-plane turbulence framework with the Leith model and the fourth-order nonlinear gradient model. A priori results show that structurally-dominated models achieve strong agreement with the ideal SGS forcing and accurately reproduce local SGS energy exchange, including backscatter. However, in a posteriori tests, structurally dominated models exhibit noise-like artifacts with high-wavenumber spectral deviations, indicating insufficient net dissipation. In contrast, the sequential DMM in which the functional component is determined first and then corrected by the structural component retains much of the a priori structural accuracy while improving the a posteriori vorticity fields, spectra, and domain-averaged diagnostics. Spectral SGS energy and enstrophy-transfer analyses show that this sequential DMM permits backscatter at scales larger than the forcing scale with enhanced dissipation at smaller scales, thereby improving the balance between instantaneous structural fidelity and long-term accuracy.

</details>


### [202] [Mesh Adaptation on Hybrid Unstructured Meshes for Immersed Boundary Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27580)
*Jonatan Núñez-de la Rosa,Esteban Ferrer,Eusebio Valero*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向浸没边界法的新预处理工具，用于在混合非结构化网格上进行网格自适应细化。该工具输入外部网格生成软件生成的非结构化、混合、保形网格，围绕浸没几何体细化网格，使采用浸没边界法的CFD求解器能高效准确地模拟流动问题。通过有限体积法和间断伽辽金法求解Navier-Stokes方程，验证了亚声速圆柱绕流和NACA0012翼型绕流，并以多段翼型高升力工况模拟作为工业应用实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浸没边界法通常依赖笛卡尔网格，灵活性有限。混合非结构化网格允许将几何体分解为固定几何部分和变化几何部分，前者用贴体网格、后者用浸没边界法处理，从而将浸没边界技术扩展到新一类流动问题。但需要一种工具在浸没几何周围进行网格细化，以保证求解精度和效率。

Method: 开发一种预处理工具，以外部软件生成的非结构化混合保形网格（包含四面体、六面体、棱柱和金字塔单元）为输入，围绕浸没几何体进行自适应网格细化。采用有限体积法和间断伽辽金法与浸没边界法耦合求解Navier-Stokes/RANS方程进行验证。

Result: 数值模拟覆盖了亚声速圆柱绕流和NACA0012翼型绕流（有限体积和间断伽辽金方法），以及工业级多段翼型高升力工况模拟（有限体积法耦合浸没边界法，RANS方程）。所有模拟结果与实验数据良好吻合。

Conclusion: 该网格预处理工具能有效支持浸没边界法在混合非结构化网格上的高精度高效模拟，且成功应用于多段翼型这类工业实际场景，验证了方法的实用性与可靠性。

Abstract: In this work, we describe a new preprocessing tool for mesh adaptation on hybrid unstructured meshes with a target application on immersed boundary methods. The tool has as input an unstructured, hybrid, and conforming mesh generated by an external mesh generation software, and the main goal is to refine this mesh around immersed geometries in such a way that the CFD solver using the immersed boundary method can simulate flow problems in an accurate and efficient manner. The input background mesh can be made of different types of elements, like tetrahedra, hexahedra, prisms, and pyramids, which, unlike Cartesian meshes, permit for a more flexible mesh. Hybrid unstructured meshes enable one to use the immersed boundary technology in a new class of flow problems where the full geometry is decomposed into a fixed geometry part and a changing geometry part. A body-fitted mesh is generated for the fixed geometry while for the changing one is used the immersed boundary method. We simulate several flow problems to test the new meshes, including subsonic flow past a cylinder and subsonic flow past an NACA0012 airfoil, both using finite volume and discontinuous Galerkin methods and solving the Navier--Stokes equations. As an industrial example of our mesh generation, we consider the simulation of a multi-element airfoil: in this case, a mesh generation software generates an unstructured conforming background mesh for the slat and main airfoil, while the flap is placed as immersed geometry in this body-fitted mesh. As accurate and efficient results are sought, this mesh is refined around the flap and then the subsonic flow at high-lift flow conditions is simulated with a finite volume method coupled with an immersed boundary method and using the Reynolds--averaged Navier--Stokes equations. The reported numerical simulations are in good agreement with experimental data.

</details>


### [203] [Compressible solved-volatility stochastic fluid thermodynamics: source-consistent energy, finite-correlation reservoirs, entropy admissibility and boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28333)
*Hsieh-Chen Tsai*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文为arXiv:2607.25536的求解挥发性随机流体公式提出了一个变密度热力学扩展。通过包含源的随机输运将质量、动量和总能守恒分解为时间演化偏微分方程与鞅相容性约束，结合有限相关库、Green-Kubo标定、平衡补偿项和伴随解析-未解析交换，实现了随机Gibbs恒等式与条件熵可容性结果。在零挥发性极限下恢复经典可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展之前的求解挥发性随机流体公式（arXiv:2607.25536），使其支持变密度和热力学效应。现有公式可能仅限于恒定密度或缺乏完整的热力学耦合，需要引入密度和温度作为原始热力学场，并通过随机粒子路径上的状态方程演化压力，以实现更一般的可压缩流体建模。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 源包含性随机输运将质量、动量、总能守恒分解为PDE和鞅约束；(2) 以密度和温度作为原始热力学场，通过质量守恒确定密度、内能确定温度、状态方程确定压力；(3) 结合解析动能恒等式、有限相关库、Green-Kubo标定、平衡补偿项及伴随解析-未解析交换；(4) 利用随机Gibbs恒等式和高斯相对熵导出Hencky库公式的条件熵可容性；(5) 区分状态方程压力涨落与机械应力冲量，采用因果有限相关载子表示快速机械压力；(6) 给出守恒边界条件和量热完美理想气体特例。

Result: 在零挥发性极限下恢复经典可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程组。冻结描述符分析识别出混合双曲-抛物型漂移子系统和代数鞅约束的耦合，具有闭包依赖的椭圆块和奇异低Mach数压力极限。规范计算验证了压力载子、声学色散、黏滞-热能量平衡和低Mach标度。作者明确不声称非线性适定性、激波可容性和充分发展的湍流性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为随机流体力学提供了统一的变密度热力学框架，将随机输运与不可逆热力学相结合，建立了从随机公式到经典可压缩NSF方程的极限对应关系。但非线性适定性、激波可容性和湍流分析等关键问题仍待解决，作为后续工作方向。

Abstract: A variable-density thermodynamic extension is developed for the solved-volatility stochastic-fluid formulation of arXiv:2607.25536. Source-inclusive stochastic transport separates mass, momentum and total-energy conservation into time-evolution partial differential equations and martingale compatibility constraints. Density and temperature are the primitive thermodynamic fields: mass conservation determines density, internal energy determines temperature, and the equation of state determines pressure evolution along stochastic particle paths. The resolved kinetic-energy identity is combined with a finite-correlation reservoir, Green--Kubo calibration, an equilibrium counterterm and adjoint resolved-unresolved exchange. A stochastic Gibbs identity and Gaussian relative entropy yield a conditional entropy-admissibility result for a Hencky-reservoir formulation. Equation-of-state pressure fluctuations are distinguished from mechanical stress impulses; regular finite-Mach fluctuations produce no independent white-noise bulk pressure impulse, while fast mechanical pressure is represented by a causal finite-correlation carrier. Conservative boundary conditions and a calorically perfect ideal-gas specialization are given. In the zero-volatility limit, the classical compressible Navier--Stokes--Fourier equations are recovered. A frozen descriptor analysis identifies a mixed hyperbolic--parabolic drift subsystem coupled to algebraic martingale constraints, with closure-dependent elliptic blocks and a singular low-Mach pressure limit. Canonical calculations verify the pressure carrier, acoustic dispersion, viscous-thermal energy balance and low-Mach scaling. Nonlinear well-posedness, shock admissibility and developed turbulence are not claimed.

</details>


<div id='nlin.AO'></div>

# nlin.AO [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Effect of biharmonic coupling in frequency-weighted Kuramoto oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27642)
*R. Senthamizhan,R. Gopal,V. K. Chandrasekar*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有频率加权双谐波耦合的全局耦合相振子动力学，通过数值分支延拓与解析稳定性分析相结合，构建了二维参数平面上的完整相图，识别出三个不同的集体态（IC、APMS、SBMS），并由三个双稳态区域和一个三稳态区域分隔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频率加权和双谐波扩展的Kuramoto模型此前已被分别研究，但二者的联合效应尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究频率加权双谐波耦合下相振子的集体动力学行为。

Method: 采用数值分支延拓与解析稳定性分析相结合的方法，在二维耦合参数平面上构造完整相图；解析推导了非相干态的线性失稳阈值，以及对称性破缺多分支态（SBMS）的存在性与局部稳定性条件。

Result: 一阶与二阶谐波相互作用的竞争产生了三种不同的集体态：非相干态（IC）、对映多分支态（APMS）和对称性破缺多分支态（SBMS），由三个双稳态区域和一个三稳态区域分隔；从非相干态到有序态的转变是不连续且具有滞后的，体现了频率加权诱导的爆炸同步。

Conclusion: 理论解析预测与广泛的数值模拟结果高度一致，揭示了频率加权与双谐波耦合联合效应下丰富的多稳态集体动力学。

Abstract: We investigate the collective dynamics of globally coupled phase oscillators with frequency-weighted biharmonic coupling. Although frequency-weighted and biharmonic extensions of the Kuramoto model have each been studied independently, their combined effect has remained unexplored. By combining numerical branch continuation with analytical stability analysis, we construct the complete phase diagram in the two-parameter coupling plane. The competition between the first- and second-harmonic interactions gives rise to three distinct collective states: the incoherent state (IC), the antipodal multibranch state (APMS), and the symmetry-broken multibranch state (SBMS). These states are separated by three bistable regions and a narrow tristable region in which all three states coexist as stable attractors. The transitions from the incoherent state to both ordered states are discontinuous and hysteretic, reflecting the explosive synchronization induced by frequency weighting, while the transition between the APMS and SBMS is also abrupt. Analytically, we derive the linear instability thresholds of the incoherent state together with the existence and local stability conditions for the SBMS, and show that the theoretical predictions are in good agreement with extensive numerical simulations.

</details>
